## T1-F03 Adobe SDK writer source evidence

The [local ticket](tickets.md#t1-f03-adobe-xmp-toolkit-writer-source-review) preceded the review. The publisher's v2025.03 tag resolves to `581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25`. The retained source ZIP hash matches the [T1 acquisition](#t1-artifact-build-and-environment-identities). Eighteen inspected SDK files match the ZIP member bytes, including the public headers, file dispatch, format handlers, reconciliation, packet parser and JPEG packager. The three retained reader binaries match the hashes recorded for T1. Those artifacts are a prior reader build and the source basis for this assessment. The review invoked no writer, reader, decoder or qualification harness. [Adobe release](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/releases/tag/v2025.03).

### Writer lifecycle and preservation

**Public API path.** `TXMPMeta` exposes namespace registration, typed property setting and localized text. A normal `TXMPFiles` edit opens for update with the format handler, obtains and edits an XMP tree, stages it with `PutXMP`, and writes on `CloseFile`. `XMPFiles.cpp:1539-1616` still calls `ProcessXMP` if the client skips `GetXMP`. The string overload parses into an XMP object. The in-place branch attempts exact packet length, then uses the normal serializer for an out-of-place update if permitted. `CanPutXMP` can return true from injection/expansion capability flags, before a file update or preservation check (`1648-1659`). Safe close uses a temporary file and format-specific update (`1230-1322`). The app's separate private copy, pre/post checks and publication gate remain necessary. [Public file API](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/public/include/TXMPFiles.hpp#L445-L491), [update implementation](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/XMPFiles.cpp#L1539-L1616).

**AR-SDK-01: JPEG smart update can change unrelated legacy metadata.** The normal `UpdateFile` calls `ExportPhotoData` before deciding whether an in-place update fits (`JPEG_Handler.cpp:668-704`). Safe-close rewrite enters `WriteTempFile`, which also calls `ExportPhotoData` unless an internal guard indicates the normal update already did it (`735-743,765-788`). Update mode initializes TIFF, IPTC and Photoshop resource writers (`534-570`). `ReconcileLegacy.cpp:127-155,183-203` exports XMP into IPTC, updates its digest/resource, exports EXIF/PSIR and removes TIFF and EXIF namespaces from the XMP tree, with specific compatibility handling. The JPEG rewrite emits the resulting EXIF APP1 and Photoshop APP13 data (`827-846,885-898`), then copies other marker payloads and the scan tail (`901-986`). The preservation risk concerns metadata even when encoded scans are copied.

A concrete discriminating case is a valid JPEG with an existing short ASCII IPTC title `Legacy title`. An otherwise permitted update sets `dc:title[x-default]` to `Image title`. The SDK maps that field to IPTC dataset 2:5 (`IPTC_Support.hpp:61`, `IPTC_Support.cpp:29-32`). `ExportIPTC_LangAlt` takes the new value, normalizes line endings and replaces the dataset when its bytes differ (`ReconcileIPTC.cpp:525-551`, `IPTC_Support.cpp:408-447,507-542`). The required `dc:description` is also mapped to IPTC dataset 2:120. These changes are outside the adopted XMP allowlist. This is a source-derived candidate counterexample. No JPEG was written for this review.

The public `kXMPFiles_OpenOnlyXMP` description suggests a reconciliation opt-out, and the JPEG handler advertises `kXMPFiles_AllowsOnlyXMP`. In the pinned source the JPEG update and close paths do not inspect the open option. The only handler uses found are in MPEG4. `skipReconcile` is private state for avoiding a second export during the same update. `kXMPFiles_OpenStrictly` does not change these write branches. The alternate packet scanner selects one existing packet and inherits a handler without injection or expansion flags (`Scanner_Handler.cpp:316-326`, `Trivial_Handler.hpp:27-29`). It cannot satisfy new XMP injection and large ExtendedXMP delivery. [JPEG handler](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FileHandlers/JPEG_Handler.cpp#L668-L788), [legacy export](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FormatSupport/ReconcileLegacy.cpp#L127-L155), [IPTC language alternatives](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FormatSupport/ReconcileIPTC.cpp#L525-L551).

**PNG path.** The handler writes in place with CRC update when the packet fits, or creates an uncompressed XMP `iTXt` chunk during a rewrite. The rewrite copies other chunks, including `eXIf`, `caBX` and image data, by chunk byte extent. The read path matches a fixed 22-byte iTXt header and returns the first matching packet. The rewrite marks matching chunks and omits them while inserting one new packet. Compressed, legacy and nested packets need separate inventory. Multiple matching packets need a preservation rule backed by source and bytes. `WriteXMPChunk` returns a failure flag that the rewrite call does not check, so a final absence/corruption check is essential even if the SDK call returns. These are source observations. The earlier Adobe PNG readback and ExifTool write controls do not qualify this SDK write path. [PNG handler](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FileHandlers/PNG_Handler.cpp#L144-L240), [PNG support](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FormatSupport/PNG_Support.cpp#L302-L402).

### Packet coverage and independent readback

- **SDK readback:** `GetXMP` exposes a handler packet and packet info. For JPEG the handler caches one main packet, groups extended portions and merges the assembly associated with `HasExtendedXMP`. Its public packet output does not enumerate all raw fragments and occurrences. On the write side, `PackageForJPEG` computes the extension MD5, puts the hex digest in the standard packet, and the handler emits offset-addressed APP1 fragments. That gives a packaging mechanism. A separate pre/post inventory must still account for original standard and extended bytes, duplicates, exact coverage and digest association. JPEG APP11 is skipped by the SDK read loop and copied by the writer's generic marker branch. PNG `caBX` is copied as a chunk. Opaque copy-through provides no original-to-logical mapping or compatibility disposition for the recognized Brotli carrier. The [AR-Q4-C1 proof](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#ar-q4-c1-coverage-proof-for-the-agreed-study) remains open. [JPEG read and merge](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FileHandlers/JPEG_Handler.cpp#L603-L649), [JPEG packaging](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPCore/source/XMPUtils.cpp#L2997-L3020).
- **Strict rejection:** `XMPMeta-Parse.cpp:944-982,1099-1102` can replace invalid UTF-8 with Latin-1 equivalents before XML parsing. The JPEG handler catches main and extension parse exceptions and can continue with other metadata (`JPEG_Handler.cpp:603-660`). A strict, namespace-aware validation pass would have to receive complete original bytes before SDK update and complete derivative bytes after it. The SDK open flags and a returned metadata object supply neither condition. [Pinned parser](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPCore/source/XMPMeta-Parse.cpp#L944-L982).
- **Independent standalone reader candidate:** ExifTool 13.55 has a separate implementation, dynamic handling of unknown XMP properties and matching-GUID extension assembly (`XMP.pm:3597-3638`, `ExifTool.pm:7559-7594`). It is plausible as a read-only check of authored standard fields and `RecordJSON` from a renamed standalone image. Later qualification would need to demonstrate config-free recovery, language alternatives, exact decoded JSON, a large multi-segment JPEG, and detection of removed or incomplete extension data. Its [known preservation and packet-coverage limits](#t1-f02-production-exiftool-release-evidence) rule out using the same readback as sole proof of unrelated metadata preservation, packet completeness or strict rejection. The retained Adobe reader remains useful prior evidence. Its use of the same SDK makes it unsuitable as the independent reader for this writer. [Pinned ExifTool assembly](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/de11d240cf7e521e939a0575a128ffe01b63be05/lib/Image/ExifTool.pm#L7559-L7594), [publisher XMP documentation](https://exiftool.org/TagNames/XMP.html).

**Disposition:** the stock v2025.03 smart-handler writer is unsuitable for the combined PNG/JPEG contract. AR-SDK-01 supplies a separate source-supported preservation conflict even if complete packet inventory were available. The packet and strict validation paths remain open, and independent readback is a candidate with explicit controls. See the [method recommendation](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#writer-recommendation-after-t1-f03). The source review establishes no runtime qualification, dependency change or T3 handoff.

**Documentation impact:** the ticket records completion, the discussion owns the method recommendation, WORK_ITEMS routes the next decision and CURRENT_STATE records the stop. The existing design, spec, phase acceptance and test evidence retain their original scope.

**Checks:** the repository document checker passed with 138 Markdown files, 6,911 local destinations and 503 verified bundled sources. Git and focused whitespace checks passed. Entry-relative hashes of 881 maintained files identify changes only to the five records above. Earlier verification bytes remain an exact prefix. HEAD and the index stayed at entry.

## T1-F04 XMP-only container feasibility evidence

The [local ticket](tickets.md#t1-f04-xmp-only-container-method-feasibility) records the scope selected on 2026-11-09 (Asia/Singapore). Entry HEAD was `a88f1bab6687a2234c689b4589b1035d8f4b6618` on `codex/cred-03`. This review inspected retained fixtures as bytes, exact publisher source, and format/API documentation. It did not exercise a decoder, metadata reader, writer or qualification harness.

### Coverage decision and exact candidate boundary

**Full packet coverage remains open.** AR-Q4-C1-R1 has a concrete source/API route for the recognized typed Brotli payloads, including a decoder interface that can prove exact byte consumption. The decisive remaining gap is a source-backed binding of compressed-box layouts to valid PNG `caBX` and JPEG APP11 JUMBF contexts and a closed traversal with original-byte provenance for all their descendants. A constructed `brob/xml ` witness or a reference to JPEG XL does not settle that binding. The old observer could record the skip while preserving native behavior. Resolving it needs a separately selected supplemental read path. No known valid class can be excluded just because the candidate writer would copy it.

**Candidate under review.** Read an immutable PNG/JPEG source, inventory original packets with the frozen ExifTool 13.61 read dispatch from the [coverage study](#coverage-study-identities-and-guidance) and the supplemental traversal below, then strictly validate each original serialization. On an opaque private derivative, a proposed container splicer would consume only an identified primary PNG XMP text chunk or a JPEG StandardXMP packet and its associated ExtendedXMP fragments, or add the primary packet when appropriate. Use Adobe v2025.03 `SXMPMeta` only for the eligible raw XMP tree and serialization, with explicit lossless JPEG partitioning and no `SXMPFiles` update. Independently reopen the physical derivative, rerun the same complete inventory and strict checks, verify the splice and use the pinned ExifTool 13.55 read-only executable for standalone value recovery. The unchanged 13.61 comparison runtime is the read-dispatch reference. Its retained read vector uses the frozen first `-config` and UTF-8 arguments, followed by `-j -G1 -s -XMP:all -EXIF:all -Warning -Error -- <absolute-image-path>`. Keep those options fixed for the baseline comparison. The supplemental path must account for original bytes even where this reporting vector omits a packet. Neither ExifTool distribution is the writer in this candidate. The instrument and splicer are proposed boundaries without corresponding implementation artifacts.

### AR-Q4-C1-R1 original bytes, fragments and decoder state

- **Start at the skipped child.** In the pinned 13.61 source, `PNG.pm:343-347,991-1028` passes `caBX` data to `Jpeg2000::Main`. `ExifTool.pm:8359-8409` recognizes `JP` APP11 and passes an assembled box to the same table. `Jpeg2000.pm:485-510,1223-1250,1323-1333` recognizes the `brob` child by the first four **child payload** bytes and calls `ProcessBrotli` with the parent `DataPt` and bounded `DirStart`/`DirLen`. Lines 1392–1420 instead read the type at parent offset zero and slice through the end of that buffer. For the retained witnesses the parent starts with `00 00 00 ca`. The earlier trace reaches the warning/early return. The source-backed supplemental edge starts at the child, not that return or a packet callback. [Pinned box dispatch and decoder caller](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/Jpeg2000.pm#L1223-L1447).
- **Static span check.** The original `brob` boxes span PNG `[74,243)` and JPEG `[47,216)`, including the eight-byte header `00 00 00 a9 62 72 6f 62`. The next four bytes are `78 6d 6c 20` and only PNG `[86,243)` or JPEG `[59,216)` goes to the decoder. Both exact 157-byte slices hash to `ec30323ee6abbd66b5abb45300dce1d0406301841a9eefa1a1d62dd9b8acaee3`. The retained generator supplies the expected 256-byte logical packet and hash. These byte reads establish the handoff, with decoder results limited to the [earlier witness evidence](#bounded-read-only-witnesses-and-exact-retained-artifacts).
- **Physical mapping before native assembly.** For PNG, retain file hash, chunk ordinal, full length/type/data/CRC spans and enclosing box tree. For JPEG, retain image/SOI identity, every APP11 marker/length, `JP` identifier, instance `En`, sequence `Z`, and original repeated `LBox/TBox` and `XLBox` when present. Build each box by image and instance with type/header consistency, and use sequence to place body ranges after one logical box header. Check bounded lengths, sequence and exact total coverage. Keep every duplicate/conflicting fragment as evidence. A logical range crossing fragments resolves to an ordered list of physical subranges. Preserve raw header spellings and any separately justified compatibility interpretation. Never derive multipart offsets from `DataPos`, reuse ExifTool's type-only bucket or lose same-type occurrences before association. ExifTool accepts a zero starting sequence with warning and has a `bmuj` endian-repair branch. Their source spans must be captured before mutation and their compatibility dispositions established separately. The JPEG committee's reference Java source reads `En`, `Z` and `XLBox`, groups by type and instance, sorts and writes one header plus fragment bodies. Its assembly gives a comparison basis, with no proof of checked bounds, source mapping or compressed-child coverage for this candidate. [Pinned JpegCodestreamParser.java, lines 126–215](https://gitlab.com/wg1/jpeg-systems/reference-software/jumbf-reference-implementation-1/-/blob/cc08dae79407fa9b5f6b6ba9de73db853081a559/jumbf/src/main/java/org/mipams/jumbf/services/JpegCodestreamParser.java#L126).
- **Decode the exact selected envelope.** Walk box bounds using the applicable normal/extended length or legal parent-end rules. Resolve the `jumd` content role and child box type. For the source-recognized typed form, take `[DirStart, DirStart+DirLen)`, require the four-byte type and pass only the remaining bytes. `xml ` yields XML bytes for XMP role identification and strict parsing. `Exif` yields a four-byte big-endian offset followed by the EXIF payload. Check the offset relative to the byte after that word and pass the bounded TIFF view to `ProcessTIFF`, retaining skipped prefix bytes and the offset interpretation. `jumb` yields the box payload for recursive child-box traversal through `ProcessJUMB`/`ProcessJpeg2000Box`. Re-enter bounded child dispatch at each recognized edge and record where each function expects payload versus header. The `ProcessBrotli` source calls these branches but labels the compressed JUMBF branch untested, and `ProcessJUMB` changes document/group state and returns success without propagating its inner traversal status. The supplemental dispatch needs isolated state and its own terminal accounting. [Pinned read paths](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/Jpeg2000.pm#L1387-L1447).
- **Compatibility is still a gate.** C2PA defines `caBX` and JPEG APP11 transport and a `c2cm` JUMBF compressed-manifest content box whose `brob` payload is the Brotli stream for an entire manifest superbox, without the witness's four-byte type prefix. JPEG XL §9.7 gives the typed Brotli grammar and differs in scope. ISO/IEC 19566-5:2023/Amd 1:2025 specifically adds JUMBF compression. The accessible publication/preview establishes its existence but did not establish the binding of the witness's XML `jumd` plus typed `brob`, or all amended compression layouts, in these two transports. The inspected JPEG committee Java revision has JPEG assembly, while its JPEG XL parser lists `brob` and skips it through the generic box path. It gives no additional closure here. Keep the typed witness in the required inventory as recognized. A context with a different `brob` grammar needs a separate proven dispatch or exact exclusion within the frozen support rules. In particular ExifTool's typed-prefix condition alone establishes only when that table branch fires. It does not certify the contents of a valid compressed manifest. [C2PA compressed boxes and embedding](https://spec.c2pa.org/specifications/specifications/2.5/specs/ContentCredentials.html), [JPEG XL format §9.7](https://www.iso.org/obp/ui?_escaped_fragment_=iso%3Astd%3Aiso-iec%3A18181%3A-2%3Aed-3%3Av1%3Aen), [ISO JUMBF compression amendment](https://www.iso.org/standard/88913.html), [JPEG committee reference source](https://gitlab.com/wg1/jpeg-systems/reference-software/jumbf-reference-implementation-1/-/blob/cc08dae79407fa9b5f6b6ba9de73db853081a559/jumbf/src/main/java/org/mipams/jumbf/services/JpegXLParser.java#L82).
- **Exact decoder candidate.** Google's official Brotli v1.2.1 tag resolves to `db24a98aed38a16de78cc05ca15cdd8dc2a0d92d`. Require terminal `BROTLI_DECODER_RESULT_SUCCESS`, all source fragments supplied, aggregate consumed length equal to the exact compressed span length, zero remaining input and all bounded output retained. Continue on output backpressure under the same budget. Exhausted input with `NEEDS_MORE_INPUT`, success with a suffix/second stream, error, missing output or exhausted resources cannot yield a complete edge. Record both sides' sizes, hashes, final state and input counters for each occurrence. This is a source/API capability. No adapter or executable hash exists for this candidate. The retained XS DLL/Perl API cannot provide this proof. [Release](https://github.com/google/brotli/releases/tag/v1.2.1), [pinned public API](https://github.com/google/brotli/blob/db24a98aed38a16de78cc05ca15cdd8dc2a0d92d/c/include/brotli/decode.h#L217-L295), [decoder exit](https://github.com/google/brotli/blob/db24a98aed38a16de78cc05ca15cdd8dc2a0d92d/c/dec/decode.c#L2875-L2905), [retained XS limit](#supplemental-traversal-and-exact-decoder).

### Remaining packet routes and completion proof

| Route | Required provenance and completion |
| --- | --- |
| JPEG StandardXMP and ExtendedXMP | Capture each Standard APP1 and every extension segment before native tag selection or hash insertion. Keep each standard serialization separate from each logical extended one. Record the 32-character GUID, declared total, per-fragment offset/length and physical spans. Reassemble by offset before decoding XML, including out-of-order fragments and split UTF-8/tokens. Require consistent total, bounded contiguous exact coverage and matching MD5 over the assembled bytes plus a matching StandardXMP `xmpNote:HasExtendedXMP` association. Record orphan, duplicate, conflicting and ambiguous cases explicitly, including ones the default reader ignores. Compare payloads and associations pre/post. JPEG marker/entropy traversal must account for multiple scans, stuffing, restart/fill markers, EOI, and source-reachable trailers or embedded images. Stock `ProcessJPEG` can stop its ordinary metadata loop at SOS or take trailer-dependent branches. Copy-through alone supplies no inventory for those branches. [Adobe storage spec §§1.1.3–1.1.3.2](https://developer.adobe.com/xmp/docs/xmp-specifications/), [pinned JPEG dispatch](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool.pm#L7494-L7660). |
| PNG textual, legacy and compressed forms | Inspect complete chunk envelopes with CRC and ordering; map iTXt keyword, flag, method, language, translated keyword and text separately, including compressed XMP text. Follow reachable `tXMP`, tEXt/zTXt, raw hex XMP/APP1/EXIF/Photoshop profiles, `eXIf`/`zXIf`, `caBX` and the recognized post-IEND continuation. Keep raw encoded bytes, validate supported method/length and exact hex grammar before interpretation, and distinguish generic XML (including `meTa` dispatch) from actual XMP. For zlib use bounded streaming with `decompressobj`, account for `unconsumed_tail`, require `eof` and exact input consumption with empty `unused_data` when the envelope specifies one stream. Use the carrier's supported compression grammar, including the actual zXIf method and declared lengths. For Nikon's `ast:XMLPackets`, identify by namespace URI and property, retain exact source spelling/spans through character-data composition, CDATA/entity handling and base64, then associate exact decoded child bytes and recurse. Consume the full grammar-permitted spelling and account for whitespace/padding without using tolerant native output as original bytes. Opaque lookalike property text stays opaque. A subrange of the current XML document is distinct from a decoded new serialization. Follow the actual table-driven edges including repeats, cycles, skips and conditional dispatch. Compressed or composed output has a mapping through the whole transform edge, without invented per-byte file offsets. Equal hashes never collapse occurrences. Close every recognized carrier with entry/exit, completion and disposition, and bind a final inventory to the input, invocation, exact source and all required dumps. Source-justified skip, genuine absence, malformed bytes, unavailable provenance, unsupported policy and resource failure have different meanings. Compare against the unchanged native traversal and independent fixture bytes. The table is a proposed coverage boundary and work map. It does not supply a completed audit of all indirect or trailer dispatch in the proposed instrument. ### Conditional write and readback analysis

- **PNG splice.** Replace only an accounted-for primary XMP text occurrence, or insert a standard UTF-8, uncompressed `iTXt` with keyword `XML:com.adobe.xmp` and empty language/translation before IDAT when creating the primary packet. Recompute only that chunk's length and CRC over type plus data. A compressed or legacy source packet requires a supported primary/duplicate treatment with before/after value correspondence. Leave untouched chunks in original order with identical payloads and framing, including `caBX`, `eXIf`, other text, ICC/color/gamma, transparency, IDAT and other image chunks. Preserve the critical chunk sequence and the placement of copied ancillary chunks relative to it. [PNG editor rules](https://www.w3.org/TR/2025/REC-png-3-20250624/#editor-behaviour), [Adobe PNG storage §1.1.5](https://developer.adobe.com/xmp/docs/xmp-specifications/).
- **JPEG splice.** Change only the identified Standard and associated ExtendedXMP APP1 marker ranges, or inject a standard/extended group at a compatible metadata insertion point after the existing leading format headers and before scans. Write APP1 marker and big-endian segment length, exact NUL-terminated standard or extension signature, and packet bytes. For each extension add uppercase MD5 GUID, full 32-bit length, 32-bit offset and fragment payload. Standard has its packet wrapper. Extended is a separate wrapper-free UTF-8 serialization. Use at most 65,002 packet bytes in Standard and 65,458 data bytes per extension segment under Adobe Part 3, with a smaller 65,000-byte slice a candidate. Preserve unrelated APP1/APP13, APP2/ICC, APP11, tables, frames, entropy-coded scans, restart markers and trailers byte-for-byte. Compare complete copied spans with before/after offset mapping. Byte equality of a copied index or signature alone does not establish continued usability. For example MPF APP2 carries offsets and lengths and a C2PA hash may cover the edited area. A source-supported placement/size plan must preserve applicable references and useful metadata. A real conflict with the XMP-only boundary would leave that case unresolved. [Adobe storage and partitioning §§1.1.3–1.1.3.2](https://developer.adobe.com/xmp/docs/xmp-specifications/), [pinned segment framing example](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPFiles/source/FileHandlers/JPEG_Handler.cpp#L851-L881), [MPF entries](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/MPF.pm#L139-L165), [C2PA hard bindings](https://spec.c2pa.org/specifications/specifications/2.5/specs/ContentCredentials.html).
- **Packet generation and preservation.** Use `SXMPMeta::ParseFromBuffer` only after strict original-byte validation, then set the app-owned allowlist by URI, including language alternatives, and use `SerializeToBuffer`. For JPEG, partition whole properties across the two trees without losing useful XMP, calculate digest over exact extension bytes, set `HasExtendedXMP` and serialize the standard packet with space reserved for that link. Verify RDF properties, qualifiers, arrays/order, language alternatives and nested serialized values across the split. The edit may change XML serialization within the chosen packet, while unedited packet occurrences retain their bytes. Keep nested/legacy occurrences in inventory and preserve them. Resolve primary selection, conflicting duplicates and useful values that a reader would mask before claiming success. Do not manufacture a passing subset by rejecting all valid affected inputs. No Adobe file update or legacy reconciliation is needed for this splice, avoiding the specific [AR-SDK-01 branch](#writer-lifecycle-and-preservation).
#### AR-SDK-02 — Oversized JPEG packaging drops XMP thumbnails

In both tree and DOM branches of the pinned `PackageForJPEG`, when the compact full serialization exceeds 65,000 bytes the code deletes existing `xmp:Thumbnails` from its standard working copy before repartitioning. It does not move that property to the extension. A useful thumbnail is outside the replacement allowlist. This is a source-derived counterexample to unconditional use of stock `PackageForJPEG`, separate from AR-SDK-01. The source-only candidate above requires an explicit preservation-aware partition. A later control pairs a small packet retaining the thumbnail with an oversized packet that must retain it as well. [Tree branch](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPCore/source/XMPUtils.cpp#L2844-L2888), [DOM branch](https://github.com/adobe/XMP-Toolkit-SDK/blob/581c41213ddcee1fbc72cbb532531102a6617a25/XMPCore/source/XMPUtils.cpp#L3068-L3093).

**Strict guards and independent reader, planned.** Feed complete original logical bytes after only verified carrier transforms to one namespace-aware Expat parser per actual XML serialization, with a final parse call and explicit carrier encoding/declaration checks. Use the previously identified Python 3.12.14 linked parser as a candidate with pinned settings and compatibility evidence. Use `ParserCreate` with namespace processing, `SetParamEntityParsing(XML_PARAM_ENTITY_PARSING_NEVER)`, no foreign DTD and an external-entity handler that fails without loading anything. Keep document/declaration policy and evidence explicit. Disallow repair, namespace fetch and source-directed I/O. Keep a policy decision distinct from XML syntax. Accept legal wrappers, BOM/padding, comments, CDATA, processing instructions and custom namespaces for the applicable carrier. A malformed input or missing coverage results in zero writer entries. Reopen and run identical checks on the actual saved private bytes, then compare unchanged spans, useful XMP and required picture properties and decoded pixels under a pinned image decoder. Require expected record equality before any acceptance. The Expat check establishes syntax only. [Agreed AR-Q4 target](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#ar-q4-bounded-qualification-target), [Expat API](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/pyexpat.html).

Use the matching pinned 13.55 Windows ExifTool executable as the independent acceptance-only reader for this Adobe Core/splice candidate. Pass an explicit empty first `-config` argument as a real argv element to disable config discovery. Use a fixed read-only query and an absolute literal path after `--` to a renamed copy of the image alone. Read all intended standard fields including language qualifiers and the unknown application namespace by its namespace identity, then decode and compare the full `RecordJSON` including exact literal escapes, identities, ordered associations and every original string. Demonstrate a large JPEG record across multiple extension segments and a failed acceptance check when a fragment or property is removed. Match against the expected record from the source facts. This reader supplies independent value recovery, while complete inventory and strict validation come from their own gates. No reader output alone proves packet coverage. [Reader source basis](#packet-coverage-and-independent-readback), [AR-8](spec.md#ar-8--source-grounded-naming-and-embedded-standalone-provenance), [AR-12](spec.md#ar-12--enrichment-fidelity-and-complete-delivery-chain).

The [next decision and bounded proposal](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#writer-recommendation-after-t1-f04) keep the coverage gate ahead of write qualification.

## T1-F05 packet coverage source gate

The 2026-11-09 request selected a ticket followed by implementation of the read-only coverage work proposed after T1-F04. The [local ticket](tickets.md#t1-f05-read-only-packet-coverage-for-the-xmp-only-candidate) was saved before the source gate was investigated. Entry HEAD was `a88f1bab6687a2234c689b4589b1035d8f4b6618` on `codex/cred-03`.

**Disposition.** The study stops at the source gate. The available material gives an exact C2PA dispatch rule and a new trailer route to account for. It still leaves the general amended JUMBF compression grammar unresolved for the chosen PNG/JPEG traversal, including the normative disposition of the typed XML `brob` witness inside an XML-described JUMBF superbox. Its native recognition keeps it in scope under AR-Q4-C1-R1. No observer, supplemental decoder adapter or writer was implemented or built. No qualification or native metadata read/write was executed. This is a source-based stop, with no runtime coverage or guard result.

### Compressed carrier evidence

- **C2PA has an exact context-specific rule.** C2PA 2.5 §5.5 and §8.5.4 identify the `c2cm` JUMBF type UUID `6332636D-0011-0010-8000-00AA00389B71`. Within that compressed manifest, the `brob` payload begins at the first compressed byte and expands to the entire standard or update manifest superbox, including its box header. Appendix A.3 binds the Manifest Store to JPEG APP11 and PNG `caBX`. A later supplemental path can select this grammar by the containing manifest role and type, give the exact payload to the candidate decoder, and verify the complete decoded box and expected manifest type/label before recursing through source-relevant descendants. Keep this role separate from the four-byte child type and payload-only result used by the typed witness. Pinned ExifTool 13.61 `Jpeg2000.pm:485-510` has only the typed-prefix selectors for `brob` and no separate `c2cm` recognition there. Correcting the buffer slice alone cannot provide C2PA compressed-child traversal. [C2PA serialization and decompression](https://spec.c2pa.org/specifications/specifications/2.5/specs/ContentCredentials.html), [ExifTool box selection](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/Jpeg2000.pm#L485-L510).
- **Reference artifacts do not settle the general rule.** The JPEG committee links its public reference dataset and C++ implementation. The pinned public dataset README identifies its JUMBF portion as ISO/IEC 19566-5:2023. The two JUMBF CSVs describe ordinary XML and JPEG, JP2, JXL and standalone cases. The reviewed CSV fields supply no PNG host or identified Brotli case with compressed-envelope and expected child information. The pinned public C++ source archive (tag `wg1n100895`) supplies no Brotli box decoder or dispatch in the inspected sources. These results describe those editions and do not establish that no later conformance material exists. ISO's published 19566-10:2026 index advertises newer electronic inserts, but the listing gives no compression inventory. The download notice conditions access to the inserts on publisher terms. The inserts were not used. The public Part 5 amendment listing and JPEG committee work plan establish the amendment's scope without giving its payload discriminator, extent and decoded representation rules. [Committee software index](https://jpeg.org/jpegsystems/software.html), [pinned dataset](https://gitlab.com/wg1/jpeg-systems/reference-software/reference-dataset/-/tree/e3e34c3b1b413e719fd5fd356107a7d7373d3fdf), [pinned C++ source](https://gitlab.com/wg1/jpeg-systems/reference-software/jumbf-reference-implementation-2/-/tree/607adbb42d9b531de182512a91e205c01cae77d5), [2026 insert index](https://standards.iso.org/iso-iec/19566/-10/ed-2/en/), [Part 5 amendment](https://www.iso.org/standard/88913.html).
- **Exact open question.** For `brob` under a non-C2PA JUMBF description, the required evidence is the amendment's context/type selection, encoded extent and resulting box or payload interpretation, including whether the XML-described witness's four-byte type prefix is permitted in each transport. Neither the public C2PA rule nor the ExifTool JPEG XL mechanism answers that question. A decoder that consumes a guessed slice cannot establish the applicable format or inventory completeness. Keep the witness as a source-recognized skipped carrier while the compatibility decision is open. [Prior typed witness mapping and decoder basis](#ar-q4-c1-r1-original-bytes-fragments-and-decoder-state).

### Additional reachable route through JPEG MIE trailers

The frozen read vector does not set FastScan. At SOS, `ProcessJPEG` invokes `IdentifyTrailer` and can call `ProcessTrailers` before its usual return. `IdentifyTrailer` recognizes a MIE trailer by the end-of-file `zmie`/extended terminator signatures. `ProcessTrailers` invokes `ProcessMIE` with `Trailer` and the file handle, and can walk preceding recognized trailers with accumulated offsets. `ProcessMIE` validates the end-relative size and walks back to the first contiguous MIE document, then calls `ProcessMIEGroup` on the main group. The `Meta` directory's `XMP` entry dispatches to `XMP::Main`. This route does not require `-ee`. Bytes copied after EOI are therefore insufficient evidence of inventory completeness. [JPEG SOS and trailer dispatch](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool.pm#L7651-L7687), [trailer identification](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool.pm#L6990-L7029), [MIE trailer entry](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/MIE.pm#L1693-L1836).

`ProcessMIEGroup` reads a bounded encoded value, inflates values with format bit `0x04`, replaces the length with decoded length and can recurse through a new in-memory random-access buffer. The downstream XMP dispatch gets the decoded bytes. It omits the physical base for a compressed value, and a nested directory may already read from a derived buffer. The observer therefore needs pre-inflate file or parent-buffer spans, per-document and per-element identities, explicit zlib completion and consumption, and the derived buffer-to-descendant mapping. Capture the original end-relative trailer association separately from each directory/packet occurrence. A future independent fixture should place an XMP value in JPEG MIE `Meta`, with a compressed counterpart and exact raw/logical spans. This is a newly specified source-route obligation, without a trailer runtime witness or a full audit of the other trailer families. [MIE metadata table](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/MIE.pm#L179-L291), [MIE inflate and child dispatch](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b/lib/Image/ExifTool/MIE.pm#L1546-L1649).

**Source identities and record scope.** The ignored `.local/ar-t1-f05-20261109-7947962a/` contains the ticket snapshot, public index and reference responses, and hashes. The JUMBF CSVs at dataset commit `e3e34c3b1b413e719fd5fd356107a7d7373d3fdf` have SHA-256 `d2381c4b19799c10d0178616ea2fd2b1f37b4b6ed003d1dfdd0c431b2bf77ea3` and `9c33b014c0aee586de78f95cd19322d2c325562dc0094ad34c5f118bfb353cdb` for the description and report respectively. C++ archive commit `607adbb42d9b531de182512a91e205c01cae77d5` has SHA-256 `147c4cd76e2166b7304f03a16ca969b2fc1eda2058225407c443e1709a3ab181`. ### Continuation source check

The subsequent continuation request resumed the source gate. The independently published MIPAMS source at commit `71b5f59590e683cf7074fa0b419397e5eed38c30` identifies the core module as `jumbf` 2.2 and the parent as `jpeg-systems` 1.2 in its POM. This is the author's BSD-licensed source release route. The review did not assume byte equivalence to the ISO electronic insert. The five inspected parser files do match the corresponding files from the earlier retained committee GitLab archive. [Author source and license](https://github.com/nickft/mipams-jpeg-systems/tree/71b5f59590e683cf7074fa0b419397e5eed38c30), [core POM](https://github.com/nickft/mipams-jpeg-systems/blob/71b5f59590e683cf7074fa0b419397e5eed38c30/jumbf/pom.xml#L8-L16).

For the retained witness's XML description UUID, `JumbfBoxService` dispatches by that UUID to `XmlContentType`. Its reader invokes `XmlBoxService`, whose expected content type is `xml ` (`0x786d6c20`). The shared `BmffBoxService` header check throws on a different TBox before reading the payload. With the witness's `brob` child (`0x62726f62`), this route provides a source-derived rejection by this implementation. It supplies no compressed XMP bytes or conformance basis for excluding the witness under the adopted support rules. The same source's JPEG XL parser lists `brob` but uses the generic skip branch for it. [JUMBF UUID dispatch](https://github.com/nickft/mipams-jpeg-systems/blob/71b5f59590e683cf7074fa0b419397e5eed38c30/jumbf/src/main/java/org/mipams/jumbf/services/boxes/JumbfBoxService.java#L82-L107), [XML content service](https://github.com/nickft/mipams-jpeg-systems/blob/71b5f59590e683cf7074fa0b419397e5eed38c30/jumbf/src/main/java/org/mipams/jumbf/services/content_types/XmlContentType.java#L19-L31), [TBox check](https://github.com/nickft/mipams-jpeg-systems/blob/71b5f59590e683cf7074fa0b419397e5eed38c30/jumbf/src/main/java/org/mipams/jumbf/services/boxes/BmffBoxService.java#L39-L79).

The JPEG committee's IANA submission and the registered `application/jumbf` type identify APP11 transport and the standalone `jumb` signature. They do not give the amended compressed-child grammar. The MP4 registration lists `brob` for JPEG XL. That entry does not determine its use inside JUMBF. The remaining input is an authoritative account of the amended layout, or conformance evidence identifying the expected compressed and decoded spans in these transports. The implementation gate therefore remains open. No reference parser or metadata tool was run. The ignored source-review root `.local/ar-t1-f05-resume-20261109-3a68dfce/` retains the archive and source scan. The archive SHA-256 is `dc0505290016f5880820ef5e243689ffc38c283b664345a9b1ec35dbf6230717`. [IANA registration](https://www.iana.org/assignments/media-types/application/jumbf), [registered box types](https://mp4ra.org/registered-types/boxes).

The user asked the agent to continue the source search directly. The published amendment's public iTeh and ANSI previews end before the compression clauses. The JPEG committee lists the third edition as a draft and the public listing supplies no substitute wire rule. DoubleBench's public Payloader description states ISO/IEC 19566-5:2023 support and supplies no amended compression layout. The C2PA public test-file index at commit `22beccc075707475b038d8789d0136c009e43143` and its image README identify conformance cases for the C2PA profile. They give no identified oracle for the non-C2PA typed carrier under review. The check covered the repository inventory and published case descriptions, with no corpus-wide binary scan. The published Part 5 amendment remains the direct source to obtain. Its public previews and the reference sources checked here leave the implementation decision unresolved. [ISO publication](https://www.iso.org/standard/88913.html), [ANSI preview](https://webstore.ansi.org/preview-pages/ISO/preview_ISO%2BIEC%2B19566-5-2023%2BAmd1-2025.pdf), [iTeh preview](https://cdn.standards.iteh.ai/samples/88913/3a283cd3dfda476fb31717ce7304817d/ISO-IEC-19566-5-2023-Amd-1-2025.pdf), [third-edition listing](https://www.iso.org/standard/91384.html), [DoubleBench Payloader](https://doublebench.com/doublebench-software/), [pinned C2PA image test inventory](https://github.com/c2pa-org/public-testfiles/tree/22beccc075707475b038d8789d0136c009e43143/2.2/image).

### Independent OpenMeta source and fixture-index check

The continuation checked a separate implementation, OpenMeta commit `9642600676ca012cfc0deea3c5e956632e2a4504`. Its JPEG XL scanner constructs `BrobU32BeRealTypePrefix` ranges by consuming four type bytes and then recording the remaining span as Brotli input. The inspected recursive JUMBF decoder has no corresponding compressed-child dispatch. Its recursion looks for nested BMFF sequences in the payload. This is source evidence for the scope of that implementation, without a format-validity conclusion for `caBX` or APP11. [Pinned JPEG XL scanner](https://github.com/ssh4net/OpenMeta/blob/9642600676ca012cfc0deea3c5e956632e2a4504/src/openmeta/container_scan.cc#L3318-L3333), [pinned JUMBF traversal](https://github.com/ssh4net/OpenMeta/blob/9642600676ca012cfc0deea3c5e956632e2a4504/src/openmeta/jumbf_decode.cc#L12560-L12594).

The OpenMeta Brotli materializer passes `avail_in` to `BrotliDecoderDecompressStream` and exits on decoder success. Its result returns output length/status without checking or exposing the final remaining input. The public path can therefore report successful output without providing the exact-consumption evidence this ticket needs. This reinforces the distinction between using the C decoder API directly with a remainder check and relying on an implementation that exposes only decoded bytes. No input was run through OpenMeta. [Pinned materializer](https://github.com/ssh4net/OpenMeta/blob/9642600676ca012cfc0deea3c5e956632e2a4504/src/openmeta/container_payload.cc#L279-L361).

The pinned C2PA public test-file tree was checked for JPEG/PNG blobs beneath `2.2/image/`. That subtree contains case documentation and empty fixture directories, with zero matching image blobs. Images under the separate legacy subtree are not evidence of the 2025 amendment. The zero count is a repository path result, without a binary conformance scan. Current ExifTool remote HEAD still resolves to the same 13.61 commit used by the study. The new evidence narrows two leads but leaves the published compression rule or an independent byte oracle as the required input. Retained source and checks are under `.local/ar-t1-f05-public-check-20261109-0a1675e6/`. Implementation remains at the source gate. [Pinned C2PA image index](https://github.com/c2pa-org/public-testfiles/tree/22beccc075707475b038d8789d0136c009e43143/2.2/image), [ExifTool 13.61 source](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/tree/9b8664dfcf2c609d3cc8925d380e98ad2a379f9b).

## T3a portable-link save implementation and verification

2026-11-09, Asia/Singapore. The user selected [T3a](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3a) implementation. **Change.** `Documents.edit` in `src/pdfmd/documents.py` replaces the embed-only HTML scan with the shared `dependencies(analysis, registered)` resolver, so local image hyperlinks and embeds both enter `assets`, with their associated `originals`, under existing hash paths. `source_registrations` groups registrations by `asset_path`. The latest non-diagnostic registration supplies a path, matching the former last-registration-wins index, and a path backed only by `purpose: "saved-output diagnostic preview"` registrations is rejected. Rejections name the destination, role, block and retained candidate, including the unresolved-handle message. `ImageLinks`/`image_links` were removed. The resolver docstring now names the T3a/T3b split. The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#shared-asset-reference-interpretation-and-selected-version-view) explains the mechanism.

**Engine checks.** The new `tests/engine/documents/test_portable_link_save.py` covers:

- a hyperlink-only save containing exactly that asset, with no unrelated registered render;
- a mixed save with repeated, linked-thumbnail and raw HTML references, keeping the Markdown exact and the hyperlink and image roles intact;
- a hyperlinked extracted `rgb-smask` image carrying both original streams;
- the A03-shaped regression fixture in link-only and inline-image variants;
- rejection of missing, foreign-task, duplicate-attribute, unresolved-handle and span-less references, with the candidate retained exactly and the head unchanged, followed by a corrected save;
- diagnostic-only paths rejected for both roles, while a later diagnostic registration sharing a source hash path leaves the source registration in force;

Two existing AR-7 controls in `test_asset_references.py` previously relied on the embed-only save to produce a historical incomplete version. They now build that fixture explicitly with `legacy_embed_only`. In `tests/desktop/fixtures/asset_references.py`, the T2 desktop fixture builds its incomplete control with `snapshot(..., inventory=False)` instead of the production save. The historical expectations themselves are unchanged. `tests/engine/documents` passed (110 tests). The implementation record does not identify a full-engine-suite result.

**Desktop checks.** A fresh `pnpm build` was used. `tests/desktop/specs/portable-link-save.spec.ts` with `tests/desktop/fixtures/portable_link_save.py` saves link-only and mixed A03-shaped sections through `edit_document` in an isolated workspace, using a page render and an extracted image with a soft mask. - used Return without a version read;
- closed and relaunched, repeated those steps and exported each version (`ZIP bytes verified. Asset references: ok`).

The verifier confirmed the versions were unchanged and the ZIP members and hashes matched. After extraction under `relocated/`, every Markdown/HTML `assets/` destination equalled the saved inventory and decoded, both originals were present, there were no protocol URLs or leases, and there were no provider requests. ## T3a implementation review and residuals

2026-11-09, Asia/Singapore. Reviewed the schema 1 save, shared resolver, screenshot eligibility, associated-original collection, candidate retention and selected-version export against [T3a acceptance](spec.md#t3a-portable-link-acceptance) and [AR-2](spec.md#ar-2--rejection-and-candidate-preservation). The code was the working tree on `codex/cred-03` at HEAD `a88f1bab6687a2234c689b4589b1035d8f4b6618`. SHA-256 was `3e06cd1cc88c153ae7b0d0d634cc5bff58ee478b49be763d7022280e54151a6d` for `documents.py` and `c16c4a52c41d9915e954423b915be0655f678ce6e5190db3355b877b02f44792` for `asset_references.py`.

- **AR-T3A-01 (P2), unrelated relative citation blocks publication.** A synthetic `edit_document` request with a valid page-render hyperlink and a Notes hyperlink to `appendix/100%-coverage.md` rejected with “Use the exact registered asset URI” for the Notes link. The candidate stayed exact and head did not advance. The normal renderer already escapes the literal percent, and the control with explicit `%25` saved with only the page image in the inventory. `classify` rejects malformed percent syntax before determining whether a hyperlink belongs to the reserved asset path space. This breaks the existing behavior for ordinary relative citations. Keep strict validation for image sources and reserved asset variants, while preserving ordinary relative citations. [Destination policy](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#supported-destinations), [classification source](../../../src/pdfmd/asset_references.py#L378-L408).
- **AR-T3A-02 (P3), corrupt registered target has no corrective context.** Corrupting the private test object for a valid image hyperlink yielded only “Saved object failed its integrity check.” The exact candidate and prior head were preserved. The save could identify the failing asset and its reference role at the display-copy boundary, and advise obtaining a fresh source image. Apply equivalent context for failures reading associated originals. [Copy boundary](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L253-L256), [object integrity check](../../../src/pdfmd/storage.py#L165-L170).

The 110 document-engine tests passed. The single T3a Playwright test passed against the existing Windows development build in a fresh profile and workspace. Both save variants reopened, navigated, returned and exported ZIPs that resolved after relocation. The desktop run report is under `.local/test-runs/20261109094915164-13508/portable-link-RjcDea/`. The two targeted reproductions are under `.local/test-runs/t3a-review-jocexr_w/`. The review made no provider calls and ran no full-engine suite, external destination matrix or writer qualification. The scoped portable-link repair is supported by these observations. The findings above remain open before using this path as the enriched-save baseline.

**Documentation impact.** Linked the findings from the ticket and continuation route and narrowed the engine guide's diagnostic claim to the checks that supply reference context. The adopted policy and Stage B completion conditions still apply. The review recommends a bounded T3a correction and an independently selected T1-F06 qualification. No application code or Git refs changed.

## T3a corrupt-object recovery correction

2026-11-09, Asia/Singapore. The user selected the fix for the AR-T3A-02 recovery residual from the [correction follow-up review](#t3a-correction-follow-up-review). This stays within T3a. Tested working tree on `codex/cred-03` (Git was unavailable in this shell): `storage.py` `d3b0c78bd9f537d8299c5ce687e28fba61b25392875947e72b5c6fabbf62d8b0`, `documents.py` `b5ac971960a6bc360625cd6b5b55fc2c48e41c75d3d856dadbb16a631d5d7280`. No provider call, writer, dependency or original-task change.

- **Object repair.** `Store.blob` (`src/pdfmd/storage.py`) no longer treats an existing `objects/<sha256>` file as proof of its content. It skips the write only when the file has the expected size and SHA-256. A missing, unreadable or corrupt file, including same-length corruption, is rewritten from the regenerated bytes: they are staged in a temporary file, the stage is verified, and `os.replace` swaps it in. If the stage fails verification or the replace fails, the existing file is left unchanged and a precise error is raised. An intact object is never rewritten.
- **Missing original record.** The original-stream record lookup in `Documents.edit` is now inside the contextual handler. A missing record names the destination, role and block, without the internal reference.
- **Guidance.** Every dependency failure now gives the recovery that works. Repeat the same `render_page` or `extract_image` call: identical bytes restore the object and register under the same asset path. Then retry the retained candidate. Evidence made with different settings gets a new path that the edit must reference.

**Rechecks.** `tests/engine/documents/test_portable_link_save.py`:

- **Recovery matrix.** A page render, an extracted display image and a retained original were each damaged three ways (appended bytes, same-length byte flip, deleted file). Each case rejects first with the recovery guidance. The same call is then repeated: the same asset path comes back, the object bytes are restored exactly, and reusing the retained candidate saves with matching display and original bytes. No temporary files are left behind.
- **Missing original record.** The rejection names the reference without the internal ID, and re-extraction followed by a candidate retry saves both originals.
- **Intact object.** A duplicate write leaves an intact object's bytes and modification time unchanged.

`tests/engine/documents` passed (133 tests). Desktop reruns of `portable-link-save`, both `asset-references` shells and `pdf-delivery` passed, 4 tests. An engine-wide rerun on this storage edition was stopped and superseded by the run recorded under the [store failure-path correction](#t3a-store-failure-path-correction), which also covers this change.

**Assessment.** AR-T3A-02's recovery residual is resolved for schema 1 saves in the tested cases. Corrupt or deleted objects can be restored by regenerating identical evidence, and the retained candidate then saves. Not established: concurrent readers holding the object open on Windows during replace, which is reported as a precise error but not exercised, and the enriched T3b path, which must recheck this behaviour. The duplicate-write check now hashes an existing object in full. This is an unmeasured cost connected to [R11](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#runtime-and-engineering-inventory). The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#shared-asset-reference-interpretation-and-selected-version-view), [T3a ticket](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3a), [WORK_ITEMS](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#context-capability-and-delivery-work) and [CURRENT_STATE](../../CURRENT_STATE.md#focus-and-resume-route) were updated.
## T3a storage recovery review

2026-11-09, Asia/Singapore. Reviewed the [corrupt-object recovery correction](#t3a-corrupt-object-recovery-correction) and its effect on shared `Store.blob` writes. The inspected `storage.py` and `documents.py` SHA-256 hashes match the correction record. All 133 document-engine tests passed independently, including the nine recovery cases and the missing-original-record case. The retained Playwright result and four case artifacts under `.local/test-runs/20261109112133529-35096/` support the reported desktop pass, with unchanged saved versions, successful relocation and zero provider requests in the portable-link case. The desktop cases were not rerun during this review. The broader engine-group run was still pending at the review checkpoint.

**AR-T3A-02 resolved in the tested schema 1 recovery path.** A real Windows read handle opened without delete sharing blocked replacement of a corrupt object. The call gave the retry message, kept the existing bytes and registration, and removed the stage. Closing the handle and retrying restored exact bytes. This checks one sharing mode on this host. [Windows file sharing](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/desktop/api/fileapi/nf-fileapi-createfilew).

**AR-T3A-03 (P3), a failed staged write leaves an orphan file.** An injected `fsync` failure preserved the existing object and record but left a temporary file. The cleanup covers failed verification and replace but does not run when the stage write, flush or sync raises. Repeated staging failures can leave untracked object copies. Keep cleanup around the entire stage lifetime and retain the original failure. [Staging boundary](../../../src/pdfmd/storage.py#L146-L162).

**AR-T3A-04 (P3), concurrent repairs can report failure after the object is restored.** Paused one repair after staging, let a second repair finish, then opened a Windows read handle on the now-correct object before resuming the first repair. The first call reported that restoration failed even though the target already held the verified bytes. No corrupt bytes were published and neither stage remained. Recheck the target when replacement fails and accept the repair only if its size and digest match. Preserve the real sharing failure when the target is still invalid. [Replacement and verification](../../../src/pdfmd/storage.py#L155-L183).

**R11 evidence.** A duplicate write of an 8 MiB synthetic object added approximately 8 MiB of peak traced Python allocation during the call, consistent with reading the full existing object to hash it. This isolates an allocation cost. It does not measure representative latency or whole-task impact. Keep [workload measurement](../../reviews/RUNTIME_FINDINGS.md#r11--verification-cost-and-long-term-storage-growth) separate from the corrective work.

Reproductions and the allocation sample are retained under `.local/test-runs/t3a-store-review-_vqkji7v/`. Recommend bounded store failure tests and fixes for AR-T3A-03/04 before accepting the shared failure path. T1-F06 remains independent. The ticket, work register, guide and continuation records now link the recovery evidence and residuals. No application code, Git refs or qualification artifacts changed.


## T3a store failure-path correction

2026-11-09, Asia/Singapore. The user selected the fixes for AR-T3A-03 and AR-T3A-04 from the [storage recovery review](#t3a-storage-recovery-review), followed by T1-F06. `storage.py` SHA-256 is `b3b23187049fb17366f1c413280b7394a383f945a9f1a194dfc886731e792782`. Git was unavailable in this shell.

- **AR-T3A-03 corrected.** `Store._restore` wraps the whole stage lifetime (create, write, flush, `fsync`, verification and replace) in one `finally` that removes the temporary file. The original exception still propagates. The existing object and record are unchanged.
- **AR-T3A-04 corrected.** When `os.replace` fails, the target is checked again. If it already holds the verified size and SHA-256, because a concurrent repair restored it, the call succeeds. Otherwise the sharing failure is reported, with the original `OSError` as its cause.

**Rechecks.** `test_portable_link_save.py` adds three tests:

- an injected `fsync` failure leaves the corrupt object untouched, leaves no stage behind and raises the injected error, and a later retry restores the object;
- a blocked replace after a concurrent restore succeeds, with exact bytes and no stage;
- a blocked replace with the target still corrupt raises the retry error, with `PermissionError` as its cause, and leaves the corrupt bytes in place.

`tests/engine/documents` passed (136 tests). Every engine group then passed separately on this storage edition: context (1,077 s), diagnostics (84 s), execution (268 s), transport (261 s) and documents (43 s), with logs under `.local/t3a-followup/`. R11 remains a separate measurement item.

## T1-F06 primary-XMP qualification

2026-11-09, Asia/Singapore. The user selected [T1-F06](tickets.md#t1-f06-primary-xmp-writer-and-independent-reader-qualification) after the store fixes. On whether ExifTool 13.61 should be used, the answer recorded is: no for acceptance. The [T1-F02 evidence](#t1-f02-production-exiftool-release-evidence) shows that the publisher designates 13.55 as production and 13.61 as a development release. The design requires production artifacts when selecting a dependency, and ExifTool is only the independent read-only reader here. 13.61 was run as a supplementary cross-reader. Its cumulative changes include a read-side XML parsing fix. See the readback clarification below.

**Candidate and tested identities.**

| Item | Identity |
| --- | --- |
| Writer, splicer and validator | `tools/qualification/primary_xmp.py` ([mechanism](../../../tools/qualification/README.md)), SHA-256 `3099c4eb9fbf973dbb1dc8fd37a7577e642ac1d91bc463fb3ac195516ad14cdc`. Python 3.12.14 standard library only, Expat `expat_2.8.3`. |
| Harness | `tools/qualification/qualify_primary_xmp.py`, SHA-256 `897c8746740f776e6dc5a6082eacda329c06f271074df3a79255ef3a2ddd6f83`. |
| Independent reader | A verified copy of production ExifTool 13.55 `exiftool.exe`, SHA-256 `377499fc2e27f8a890714c6734440646141bd821ca4c305092bd6b6326b4ff49`, matching the [T1 artifact record](#t1-windows-helper-qualification). Run with `-config ""`, so no config is loaded. |
| Cross-reader | ExifTool 13.61, SHA-256 `29121f6ecdebdb91abb1f06614c4c5a74746455a6a1c6b00c16dd093164e5be7`. Supplementary only. |
| Decoder | PyMuPDF `2.0` binding and MuPDF `1.28.5`. |

Every preparation and reader call ran under the pre-start Job (256 MiB memory, five seconds CPU, 15 seconds wall): 80 calls, with peak Job memory at most 44 MiB for preparations and at most 1.1 s wall. The run is retained under `.local/t1-f06-20261109-194858/`. Earlier runs `…194327`, `…194443`, `…194643` and `…194748` were harness and candidate iterations; the corrections are listed below. No provider call, application integration, dependency or lockfile change was made.

**Result: 31 of 31 controls passed.**

- **Accepted inputs (14).** Each passed every check:
  - the candidate accepted it;
  - the input was unchanged;
  - non-XMP chunks or segments were byte-equal in order, and also in the independent payload-identity comparison;
  - decoded pixels were equal;
  - the image was copied and renamed alone (`中文% independently renamed`) into an empty folder;
  - ExifTool 13.55 recovered the complete `RecordJSON`, equal to the expected record, plus `dc:title`/`dc:description` in `x-default` and `zh-CN`, `dc:source` and the three `pdfmd` identifiers;
  - unrelated reader values were equal before and after, with no new diagnostics;
  - the 13.61 cross-read returned the same record.

  The inputs were:
  - MuPDF `render_page` PNG and three decoded `extract_image` PNGs (soft mask, CMYK+mask, and JPX with `iCCP`);
  - the unchanged PDF DCT stream in create mode;
  - a merge into an existing complex primary record: a second `rdf:Description`, attribute-form properties, language alternatives, a Bag, a Seq, a CDATA/comment custom property, a struct, a stale `pdfmd:RecordJSON` and `xmp:Thumbnails`;
  - AR-SDK-01, where APP13 IPTC ObjectName differs from the XMP title and APP13 stayed byte-identical with IPTC still readable;
  - AR-SDK-02, existing thumbnails plus a long record: 23 extension fragments, thumbnails preserved;
  - a long record created fresh: 23 fragments with UTF-8 characters split across fragments;
  - EXIF with a `../outside/source.jpg` filename tag plus ICC;
  - an APP11 JUMBF `brob` box, byte-identical and with no decoding needed;
  - an existing ExtendedXMP input with fragments in reversed order, merged and repartitioned into 2 fragments.
- **Input rejections (14).** Each failed before any output, with its specific code, no output or stage file, and the input unchanged:
  - `png_unexpected_xmp`, `png_unexpected_carrier` (`caBX`) and `unsupported_type` (TIFF bytes named `.png`);
  - `mpf_unsupported`, `competing_primary`, `malformed_xml`, `invalid_utf8` and `dtd_forbidden`. The last was an external-entity DOCTYPE, rejected without any resolution.
  - ExtendedXMP `gap`, `overlap`, `incomplete`, `digest_mismatch`, `orphan_extension` and `guid_mismatch`.
- **Injected output defects (3).** Each was caught at the postcheck with no published output: a copied-byte flip (`png_crc`), a dropped extension segment (`incomplete`) and a renamed allowlisted property (`preserved_property_changed`).
- **Resource-limit control.** A 400 MiB allocation under the Job failed, as required.

**Findings during qualification.**

1. **Stale nested record shown by the reader (resolved by policy).** A JPEG with no primary XMP but an old packet in Photoshop APP13 resource 0x0424 was first accepted. ExifTool 13.55 then returned the stale nested `dc:title` from the delivered image, which fails standalone readback. The candidate now rejects such inputs with `legacy_xmp_competes`, and the same applies to XMP in EXIF IFD0 tag 0x02BC. This is the adopted rule to stop when a legacy top-level packet may compete. It is a compatibility gap: a PDF DCT stream carrying Photoshop-embedded XMP cannot be enriched under this policy. Its [decision record](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t1-f06-qualification-result-and-selection-questions) asks whether to keep it.
2. **Reader misreads comments in the input.** ExifTool 13.55 misread the input record's comment containing `</my:Note>`, reporting "XMP format error" and truncating the value. The candidate now reserializes preserved properties without comments and with CDATA escaped. The output reads cleanly with the data-model value `alpha <tag> &  tail`, while the strict-parser model comparison is unchanged. The harness checks that value by construction instead of trusting the reader's reading of the input.
3. **Harness corrections, with no candidate behaviour change.** Three corrections were made:
   - the Chinese path reaches ExifTool through a UTF-8 `-@` argument file;
   - long values are read with `-b` and binary values compared by digest;
   - diagnostics already present on the input (`[minor] Unknown Brotli box type`) count as inherited.

**13.61 readback clarification (2026-11-09).** The [13.58 changelog entry](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/exiftool/exiftool/13.61/Changes) identifies the XML comment and CDATA parsing correction carried into 13.61. In the retained focused AR-Q1 runs, the four `*-valid-taglike-*-before-full-readback/stdout.bin` calls per arm read the original PNG/JPEG inputs before rewriting. The 13.61 arm returns `before after` for the comment cases and `before </xmp:Label> after` for the CDATA cases. The 13.55 arm returns `before<!-- ` and `<![CDATA[before ` respectively. These are direct read-side results on the earlier fixtures. The final T1-F06 13.61 cross-read operates on the reserialized output, so it supplies no direct 13.61 observation of the exact original `my:Note` fixture.

The earlier T1-F06 run `.local/t1-f06-20261109-194443/` also retains a 13.61 read of the standalone JPEG containing both the new primary packet and the stale Photoshop APP13 packet. `calls/jpeg-stale-nested-cross-read-13.61/stdout.bin` (SHA-256 `2172c00a2aaee3ec8580b09b222ff2ecf47c62bb1a95de671366f1d44fabfec5`) returned `XMP-dc:Title = STALE nested title` with exit 0. The expected default title was `测试图像`, and 13.61 did recover the new `XMP-pdfmd:RecordJSON`. The standalone image has SHA-256 `58b4198ed87d4bd34483909c14df9ca08fede22efb1814b0dacb70b11927df0c`. This directly establishes the remaining APP13 title conflict on 13.61 for the tested image. EXIF `0x02BC` remains a separate rejection control. This clarification uses retained artifacts and leaves the qualification scope and production-reader selection boundary intact.

**Disposition: qualified for the bounded tested preparation on Windows development.** This covers `primary_xmp.py` at the hash above as writer, splicer and validator, with ExifTool 13.55 as independent reader, for the tested inputs:
- MuPDF-generated PNGs;
- unchanged PDF DCT JPEGs, with or without an existing primary or ExtendedXMP record.

Not established:
- product selection, which is a [separate decision](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t1-f06-qualification-result-and-selection-questions);
- the app's producer-eligibility check from registration metadata, which belongs to T3b;
- other platforms, external viewers and readers;
- real PDFs with publisher-embedded XMP;
- performance at scale;
- C2PA signature and asset-binding validity, reported as unverified by design.

T3b remains blocked until a candidate is explicitly selected for product use.

## T1-F06 B-lite legacy-XMP requalification

2026-11-10, Asia/Singapore. The user chose [B-lite](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t1-f06-qualification-result-and-selection-questions) for legacy JPEG XMP and kept ExifTool 13.55 as the acceptance reader. This changes the rejection in finding 1 of the run above. That run, its hashes and its disposition remain the record for the earlier candidate.

**Candidate change.** `primary_xmp.py` now locates APP13 `0x0424` and EXIF IFD0 `0x02BC` values by absolute byte range. Before the primary scan, it overwrites each with an empty XMP packet of the same length. A legacy value is accepted only after strict XML and XMP-structure parsing. The postcheck independently re-blanks the input and requires:
- every byte that differs between input and blanked input to lie inside a legacy value range;
- the remaining copied ranges to equal the output;
- every legacy carrier in the output to equal the empty packet exactly.

A new injected defect, `legacy-residue`, alters one padding byte of a blanked value in the staged output.

**Tested identities.** Candidate SHA-256 `74c30f2be61aec0de6baacabb89ec2c73f45b2acddfcb272914c2ea78220d6a2`. Harness SHA-256 `123126386a6c6f41e0475b7c2cfd0ab658b784944f1dde02398e00328ced198e`. Readers, decoder, Python and Expat are the same as the earlier run: ExifTool 13.55 for acceptance and 13.61 as cross-reader, both copied from `.local/t1-f06-20261109-194858/`. The run is retained under `.local/t1-f06-blite-20261110-162646/`: 103 Job calls, peak Job memory at most 43.4 MiB for preparations and at most 1.23 s wall. No provider call, application integration, dependency or lockfile change was made.

**Result: 38 of 38 controls passed**, on the first run of this edition. They are the earlier 31 controls plus seven new ones; two earlier rejection controls now expect acceptance.

- **Accepted legacy inputs (4).** Each passed the existing accepted-input checks except byte equality outside the primary group. A harness check replaces that: it finds the known legacy packet in each changed payload and requires the same length, identical bytes before and after it, and an empty `x:xmpmeta`/`rdf:RDF` body with no legacy marker. The legacy packets carried a stale `dc:title`, a stale `pdfmd:RecordJSON` and a legacy-only `my:Legacy` value.
  - `jpeg-stale-nested`: APP13 with IPTC 0x0404 and XMP 0x0424 (651 bytes blanked). IPTC ObjectName stayed readable and unchanged.
  - `jpeg-exif-0x02bc-xmp`: little-endian EXIF, BYTE type (655 bytes).
  - `jpeg-legacy-exif-mm`: big-endian EXIF, UNDEFINED type.
  - `jpeg-legacy-both-with-primary`: EXIF and APP13 legacy packets plus an existing primary record, merged.

  For all four:
  - ExifTool 13.55 and 13.61 both returned `XMP-dc:Title = 测试图像` and the complete new record;
  - the `-a -G1:3` all-documents read contained no stale or legacy-only value;
  - the only reader key that disappeared was `XMP-my:Legacy`, which is discarded by design.
- **New input rejections (4).** Each failed with its specific code, no output or stage file, and the input unchanged: `legacy_xmp_too_short` (a 36-byte packet), `legacy_malformed` (unclosed XML), `legacy_unparseable` (an IRB size exceeding its APP13 segment) and `legacy_unparseable` (0x02BC with SHORT type).
- **New injected defect (1).** `legacy-residue` was caught at the postcheck as `legacy_xmp_competes`, with no published output.
- The other 29 earlier cases and the separate resource-limit control behaved as before.

**Disposition.** Qualified for the same bounded scope as the earlier run, now including JPEGs with a legacy XMP value in APP13 `0x0424` or EXIF IFD0 `0x02BC`. The not-established list above still applies. Two additional limits:
- XMP in other locations that whole-file readers may scan, such as a MIE trailer, is neither blanked nor rejected;
- real publisher JPEGs with Photoshop-embedded XMP have not been run.

Product selection for T3b is still open, so T3b remains blocked until a candidate is selected.

**Document checks.** The design boundary, batch spec controls, decision record, tickets, tools README and CURRENT_STATE were updated for this change. `tools/docs/check_docs.py` reported 0 errors (140 Markdown files, 7,199 links and destinations).

**Engine observations (18 tests, all passing).**
- **Selection and invocation.** The engine module hash equals the selected identity and the qualified file. For a PNG render, a DCT JPEG and a long ExtendedXMP record, in-process `splice` plus `postcheck` output equals the `prepare` command output byte for byte.
- **Create.** One save creates a render PNG and an unchanged DCT JPEG with:
  - names `新聞基本屬性： 示意／比較-p58-圖4–2.jpeg` (substituted `:` and `/`) and `新聞基本屬性-p58.png`;
  - handles rewritten to encoded URIs in operative spans only, with identical literal text in code and comments unchanged;
  - image and hyperlink roles preserved;
  - exactly the two deliverables plus the DCT original in the version;
  - reference check `ok`;
  - decoded pixels equal to the pre-enrichment evidence, while the container hash changed;
  - the embedded `RecordJSON` equal to the catalog and inventory record, with the printed label, its basis and the pre-enrichment and original hashes.

  Replay returns the original receipt. Export, relocation and a renamed lone copy keep the record.
- **Distinct occurrences and collisions.** Two creates from the same image get separate occurrence IDs and `…-p58.png` / `…-p58（2）.png`. An English-style ` (2)` is a different complete name. A case-only variant collides through the comparison key and takes ` (3)`.
- **Reuse and removal.** `asset:id/<occurrence>` reuses the same path and bytes. Removing the final reference drops the asset from the new version only, while the earlier version keeps its file. The name stays owned, so a later create with the same label gets `（2）`.
- **Labels.** A legacy untouched label `xii` yields `Overview-i1.png` and `legacy_hint: "xii"` with unknown state.
- **Legacy adoption.** An unadopted schema 1 hash path in a schema 2 save fails `ASSET_NOT_OWNED` with adoption guidance. `legacy_path` adoption rewrites both the embed and the link to the new URI.
- **Rejections.** Each of the following leaves head unchanged and keeps the exact candidate, with `error_code` and `issues`; a later valid create gets the unsuffixed name, showing the reservation was released:
  - a diagnostic preview, an out-of-source location page, a printed label without basis and a `correct` request (`ASSET_FACT_INVALID`);
  - an unknown handle (`ASSET_REFERENCE_INVALID`);
  - an overlong label (`ASSET_NAME_BUDGET`, with budget details).

  Foreign-task evidence fails `ASSET_NOT_OWNED` without revealing the other task.
- **Failures and recovery.** A stale head fails `HEAD_CONFLICT`. A version-file write failure publishes nothing, and the name stays free for the next attempt. A simulated crash after reservation leaves a pending name that another writer cannot take (it receives `（2）`); retrying the same operation resumes with the same occurrence ID and name and commits.
- **Name boundaries.** These cover the nine substitutions and a tab, a containing-reserved-word name left unchanged, a range, mixed `&`, the empty-label fallback, NFC, exactly 180 bytes accepted, 181 rejected with exact details, suffix growth overflow, full case folding (`Straße`/`STRASSE`), canonical equivalence and width distinction.
- **HTTP.** The same `Idempotency-Key` returns the original receipt with one version. Reusing it with different arguments and sending an invalid key both fail.

**Integrated standalone readback.** Run `.local/t3b-readback-20261110-03/`, on the final code: version `2e4b430aa5f342488be4e6777902ceb9`, reference check `ok`, 6 Job calls, peak 52.7 MiB, at most 1.3 s. The save covers both images, a book source with title and citation, and a 27,000-character mixed-language description with CR/LF, `&`, `<` and `>`; the JPEG uses ExtendedXMP. Each image passed:

- `RecordJSON` equals the saved record;
- `dc:title`, `dc:source` and `pdfmd:OccurrenceId` match, and the citation appears in `dc:description`;

- 13.61 returned the same record.
