The local snapshot `.local/t3b-baseline-20261110/` separated T3b changes from earlier T2/T3a work. The working writer, document implementation and schema match their [first-increment hashes](#t3b-first-increment-enriched-creation). The adopted [fact and representation rules](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#versioned-data-and-embedded-schema), [operation and publication contract](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#document-edit-api-and-operation-semantics) and [AR-8 through AR-12](spec.md#ar-8--source-grounded-naming-and-embedded-standalone-provenance) govern the assessment.

- **AR-T3B-01 (P2), accepted image provenance can contradict source evidence.** Through the normal edit Dispatcher in a two-page synthetic task, a page-1 render saved with its own image reference presented as evidence for page 2. The saved name was `Actual first page-i2.png`. Embedded provenance recorded location page 2 and image derivation page 1. A separate edit with nonexistent evidence references for a known printed label and source title also saved. The occurrence check verifies ownership and page bounds but does not compare evidence page/region with recorded derivation. Source and printed-label facts bypass even the occurrence ownership check. Validate evidence type, task and location where the recorded data can establish them across all embedded fact scopes. Require support for a distinct repeated occurrence without forcing every caption or bibliographic pointer to the image page. Use mismatched and dangling evidence as rejection controls alongside genuine repeated-occurrence and separate-caption controls. [Occurrence validation](../../../src/pdfmd/asset_catalog.py#L85-L129), [source and page preparation](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L224-L242).
- **AR-T3B-02 (P2), a null source patch can save an unusable base.** A text-only edit with `source_update: { "title": null }` published a schema 2 version containing a null title. A subsequent normal asset creation on that base failed with `'NoneType' object is not subscriptable`. The patch model accepts explicit null even though the compact schema omits it and the stored field requires a complete `TextFact`. The same unchecked merge also accepts null list fields. Reject supplied null for non-nullable fact fields at input, keep omission distinct from an empty list and from an explicit unknown fact, and verify that a base already produced with a null can be repaired with a valid patch. [Source patch model](../../../src/pdfmd/schema.py#L143-L152), [merge](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L388-L392), [XMP projection](../../../src/pdfmd/asset_catalog.py#L185-L192).
- **AR-T3B-03 (P2), retry can change a reserved name and strand prepared work.** Two interrupted operations reserved `Reserved-i1.png` and `Reserved-i1（2）.png`. After the first settled as a failure, retrying the second searched from the start and took the newly released unsuffixed name. If the second interruption occurred before enrichment, the retry published the changed name and left its original suffix pending. If it occurred after the enriched blob was staged, the same representation ID now generated different name-bearing bytes and failed with `An immutable reference already contains different data`. Head remained empty and the old suffix stayed pending. The reproductions simulated process loss at the preparation boundaries in a temporary task store. The task lock serializes active writers, while pending reservations can survive the lock holder. Persist and resume the exact allocation and prepared identity, including after a lower slot clears. On settled failure, release every reservation belonging to that operation together with the failure receipt. Recheck both interruption points and the release-to-retry interval. [Allocation and preparation](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L343-L416), [immutable enriched blob](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L471-L485).
- **AR-T3B-04 (P2, source-supported), HTTP retry identity is global and the desktop omits it.** The route builds `manual:<key>` without the task identity. The candidate and operation records share store-wide IDs, so independently valid edits to two tasks cannot both use the same caller key. The desktop bridge sends the edit body without an idempotency header, leaving ordinary manual submissions outside the new retry path. Qualify the identity by task, have the desktop keep one key per logical submission, and check same-key reuse across tasks, replay after a newer head and a lost response. The existing HTTP test covers one task only. [HTTP edit route](../../../src/pdfmd/server.py#L357-L369), [candidate identity](../../../src/pdfmd/tools.py#L73-L77), [desktop edit request](../../../src/desktop/main.ts#L177).
- **AR-T3A-02, enriched-path residual (P3).** Corrupting only the content-addressed copy of a synthetic version's enriched JPEG caused the next save to reject with `ASSET_INTEGRITY_FAILED` and preserve head. The saved version copy still verified. Re-extracting the JPEG as directed did not restore the enriched object because it regenerates pre-enrichment bytes. The diagnostic also omitted the affected link role/span and did not distinguish image from original failure. Reuse verified identical enriched bytes where a sound immutable copy exists, or give an accurate recovery instruction when it does not. Recheck the retained-original and missing-record cases when addressing the shared diagnostic. The [schema 1 correction](#t3a-corrupt-object-recovery-correction) retains its original result. [Enriched dependency read](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L520-L534).

**Checks and limits.** A fresh run of `test_enriched_save.py` and `test_portable_link_save.py` passed all 43 cases. The counterexamples above ran in disposable local stores. The review independently compared every member in the retained readback ZIP manifest and matched both retained ExifTool 13.55 `RecordJSON` results to the package inventory for version `2e4b430aa5f342488be4e6777902ceb9`. It inspected prior readback artifacts without rerunning ExifTool. The recorded six Windows Job calls measure readers only. The writer ran in-process and its qualification limits do not carry over. No fresh desktop, full-engine, frozen-build, writer-qualification, publisher-source, live conversion or destination-matrix run was performed.

**Assessment.** The successful synthetic delivery chain is supported within its tested scope. T3b is not ready to hand off as a reliable creation and recovery baseline for T4. The recommended next increment is to correct the fact validation, invalid patch and retry behavior, then recheck publication, reopen and relocated export on one identified version. The [remaining T3b work](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3b) and [open integration choices](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t3b-review-and-open-integration-choices) remain distinct from these reproduced defects. Stage B still needs its existing source-led acceptance, and this review does not select another conversion.

**Documentation impact.** Reconciled the [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b), [T3b ticket](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3b), [work dispositions](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#context-capability-and-delivery-work) and [continuation route](../../CURRENT_STATE.md#focus-and-resume-route) with the actual invocation, reviewed limits and outstanding decisions. The maintained [Windows build script](../../../tools/build/build-windows.ps1#L7-L13) already includes the complete `src/pdfmd/data` directory via `--add-data`. The earlier assertion of a missing collection rule was incorrect. Actual frozen enrichment remains unverified. The earlier evidence also describes desktop and readback as running on the final code and later dates them before schema compaction. Use the explicit run-order limitation for those observations. The README's preview behavior, project layout, adopted contracts and phase acceptance continue to apply. The repository documentation check reported zero errors. The review changed documentation only.

**Implementer recheck (2026-11-10).** The implementer independently reproduced the review findings in a disposable two-page store, with no code change:
- AR-T3B-01: a page-1 render declared at page 2 saved as `Actual first page-i2.png`, and nonexistent `printed_basis` and source-title evidence references saved.
- AR-T3B-02: `source_update: {"title": null}` saved, and the next enriched save failed with `'NoneType' object is not subscriptable`.
- AR-T3B-03: after `op1` settled, retrying `op2` took `Reserved-i1.png` and left `Reserved-i1 (2).png` pending under `op2`.

Source inspection confirms AR-T3B-04: `server.py` builds `manual:<key>` without the task, the Dispatcher's `candidate:` and `result:` keys are store-wide, and the desktop `edit` call sends no header. It also confirms the enriched-path AR-T3A-02 residual: the enriched dependency read reuses the source-evidence `RECOVERY` text. The implementer's earlier statement that the frozen build omits `pdfmd/data` was wrong; `tools/build/build-windows.ps1` adds it.

## T3b review correction

2026-11-10, Asia/Singapore. The user selected fixing all five findings of the [T3b first-increment review](#t3b-first-increment-review) as one T3b correction. The writer-boundary choice was not selected and remains open in the [integration choices](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t3b-review-and-open-integration-choices). The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) explains the corrected mechanisms. No provider call was made and nothing was committed.

**Corrections.**
- **AR-T3B-01.** `check_pointers` validates every evidence pointer in occurrence facts, merged `source_facts` and page-label evidence:
  - the reference must exist in this task and be an evidence-kind record;
  - the pointer's page must match the page the record's metadata establishes (image `page`, inspection `pages`);
  - its region must overlap a render's recorded `region`.

  `validate_occurrence` also requires each location to agree with the image's recorded page. A location on another page needs separate evidence recorded from that page, which covers a genuine repeated occurrence. A location region on the image's page must overlap the image's recorded region.
- **AR-T3B-02.** `SourceFactsPatch` rejects explicit null for every field. `validate_source` rejects a merged source with an invalid fact and names the `source_update.<field>` that repairs it.
- **AR-T3B-03.** `_reserve` stores each client's `{name, collision, rid}` in the preparation record in the same transaction as the claim. A resumed operation reclaims exactly that allocation, or fails `OPERATION_CONFLICT` if another occurrence owns it. `_release` releases every pending name owned by the failed operation.
- **AR-T3B-04.** HTTP operations are `manual:<job>:<key>`. The renderer keeps a per-draft `submissionKey`, renewed when the draft changes, and the main process sends it as `Idempotency-Key: desktop:<uuid>`.
- **AR-T3A-02 enriched residual.** `_enriched_bytes` restores a missing or corrupt enriched object from any saved version's hash-verified identical copy and records `enriched_object_restored`. Without such a copy, the error names the file and the destination, role and block, states that re-extraction cannot rebuild it, and directs a new create. Original-stream failures name the `originals/` path and the `extract_image` recovery.

**Tested identities (SHA-256).** `documents.py` `36d2f5b502ce498e8ddffb8f453b3be879d18e243f3c4aec0587f804436177c3`; `asset_catalog.py` `4ba8f55052e17e1b921eb685bdc86fa88a4026481cbc7a2f60d44c1e14058ca1`; `schema.py` `542c6431f70399e7c3af2eb8d34a327eb3f09f6bf04ee1f4b1de2e4a3bc97730`; `server.py` `55703717d663a57054d8b0c2c04761122817bbdc4e7deedb925908e32091c3c9`; `src/desktop/main.ts` `7da87ed2e1f1191edb0eaf22045de648291b11775248d2e92e72f247959aaf0d`; `src/renderer/main.tsx` `260ca08687502a44e99aa9d8f2c4ef3687ba88babda86fbd35453a2c53a0c6b0`; `useDocumentSession.ts` `347aa70061475a6ee9905589ed35c4a6a1d5f9cd7c4da4f050265ec4b158b36d`; `types.ts` `67e5b374b19b8a7f77c1db56ae36d2313df3ef0832ee846c3ae278277b53f0ff`. The selected `primary_xmp.py` is unchanged.

**Checks.**
- **Implementer reproductions.** The script that reproduced AR-T3B-01 to 03 before the change now shows each case rejected or resumed correctly. The page mismatch and dangling evidence are rejected, the null title is rejected, and `op2` resumes `Reserved-i1 (2).png` while `op1`'s name is released.
- **Engine tests.** `test_enriched_save_review.py` (`ab2e85d03984183621c94aded3fb0769a8e897d96466831714d55b9fca5cba92`, 9 tests) and the updated `test_enriched_save.py` (`a31c2e4952b6e75a313728bb175dfcdf4f4a2e929dff51c029f8bdcf09ef439b`): 27 passed. They cover:
  - mismatch and unsupported locations rejected, and a repeated occurrence with separate page-2 evidence accepted (`Repeated-i2.png`);
  - dangling evidence in printed-label, source and caption scopes rejected with `ASSET_NOT_OWNED`; a candidate-kind reference and an off-region pointer on a crop render rejected;
  - a caption cited from page 2 accepted for a page-1 image;
  - null `title`, `creators` and `kind` rejected; a damaged null-title base blocked with the repair field named, repaired by an explicit unknown fact, then accepting an empty-list patch;
  - interruption before enrichment and after staging, with the lower slot released in between, both resuming the original suffix; a settled failure releasing every pending name of its operation;
  - the same HTTP key in two tasks giving two versions, and a lost-response retry after a newer head returning the original receipt;
  - same-length corruption of an enriched object restored from the saved copy; the unrecoverable enriched case and a corrupt original giving their specific guidance, with head unchanged.
- **Desktop**, on a fresh `pnpm build`:
  - `document-session.spec.ts` (`6b18a62a366405ca5be876c22b685048ba7994d97e87ee26c33b0b1a3a5d74a5`) asserts a `desktop:<uuid>` key on a save. Resubmitting the unchanged draft after a head conflict reused the key, and a new rebuild draft used a different key.
  - `enriched-save.spec.ts` (`7f5328a4643ab812212a739365c94f2338a98fa4c1e459cf2109d5fc1da49663`, with fixture `5dc937469147903f980ee03353349a0f102a8721ac11b15aad5ba15da24f8c7d`) now also edits the relaunched schema 2 version through the real UI. Its verification found exactly one `manual:<job>:desktop:` operation and a new head with the same catalog. Reopen, native links, Return and the relocated ZIP passed again.
  - The T3a `portable-link-save.spec.ts` regression passed.
- **Standalone readback.** Run `.local/t3b-readback-20261110-04/`, version `18029501e8e5420084fc5bac907129a4`: both images passed every check with ExifTool 13.55, and 13.61 returned the same records.
- **Engine groups.** Every group passed separately on the corrected code: documents (61 s), diagnostics (93 s), execution (304 s), transport (262 s) and context (1,057 s). Logs are under `.local/t3b-groups/`.

**Limits.**
- Evidence relationships are checked only where stored metadata establishes them. A decoded extract records a page but no region, and tool-result references record neither.
- The desktop performs no automatic transport retry. Recovery of a lost response depends on the user resubmitting the unchanged draft.
- The writer still runs in-process. Frozen-build enrichment, cross-process concurrent allocation, publisher sources and the T5 matrix remain unverified.

## T3b bounded writer process

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. The user chose a bounded child process for the selected XMP writer, deciding the writer boundary in the [integration choices](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t3b-review-and-open-integration-choices). The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) explains the mechanism. No provider call was made and nothing was committed.

**Change.** `asset_catalog.enrich` now calls `xmp_worker.enrich_bounded`. The engine relaunches itself as a worker: the frozen executable with `--xmp-prepare`, or `python -I -B -m pdfmd.xmp_worker` in development. Before reading input, the worker joins its own kill-on-close Job with 256 MiB job memory and 5 s job CPU; the parent enforces 15 s wall time. The worker then runs the unchanged selected `primary_xmp.prepare` with the qualified arguments. The preparation folder under `<store>/preparation/` is removed after an accepted, hash-matched output and kept on failure. Rejections and process failures return `ASSET_ENRICHMENT_FAILED` and release the operation's names.

**Build-script correction.** Freezing the engine for this check showed that the maintained `tools/build/build-windows.ps1` failed at PyInstaller. Its relative `--add-data src/pdfmd/data` was resolved against `--specpath build`, giving `build/src/pdfmd/data`: "Unable to find". The line has been committed since `eae596a`, so the earlier frozen executable under `build/engine/` was stale and lacked `primary_xmp`. The script now passes the resolved absolute path.

## T3b bounded writer review

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. Standalone review of the uncommitted bounded-writer increment on `codex/cred-03` at `a88f1ba`. The current worker, document and catalog services, selected writer and Windows build script match the [bounded-writer hashes](#t3b-bounded-writer-process). The frozen engine also matches the recorded executable hash. The review covers enrichment, worker failure and resumption through `edit_document`, the Windows freeze check and the connected guidance. It uses the [writer selection](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t3b-review-and-open-integration-choices), [enriched save flow](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#enriched-save-flow) and [AR-10 and AR-12](spec.md#ar-10--collision-allocation-retries-and-distinct-occurrences).

### AR-T3B-05 — Engine exit defeats the writer wall limit

**P2, reproduced with a controlled child.** The child owns the only handle to the kill-on-close Job. Electron quit kills the engine, which owns the `subprocess.run` wall timer. In an isolated Windows probe, a child joined the production Job, signalled readiness and waited at a gate. The review terminated the parent, waited past its test-configured two-second wall limit, then opened the gate. The child still wrote its completion marker. The prepared input remained on disk. The CPU and memory limits were present throughout the wait. A blocked worker can continue after the app closes and can overlap a restarted operation. The test establishes process lifetime; it did not publish a version or reproduce an indefinite stall with a real image. [Child Job ownership](../../../src/pdfmd/xmp_worker.py#L54-L75), [parent timer](../../../src/pdfmd/xmp_worker.py#L121-L125), [Electron shutdown](../../../src/desktop/main.ts#L205), [Windows Job handle semantics](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/procthread/job-objects).

Keep the bounded writer and tie its process lifetime to the engine using an engine-owned kill-on-close Job. Assign the process before it can enter preparation. Recheck normal completion, a deadline after preparation has begun, hard engine termination, frozen launch and immediate restart of the same unsettled save. Verify worker termination, retained preparation and coherent head/reservation behavior. T1-F06 ran the selected writer under an already active Job; the current self-assignment begins after worker bootstrap. Byte equivalence and existing readback continue to support the selected writer artifact.

### AR-T3B-06 — Partial worker diagnostics mask the save failure

**P3, reproduced through `edit_document`.** A fault-injection child wrote a single `{` to `report.json` and exited with status 19. The edit settled with a raw JSON decode error and no `error_code`. Head stayed unset, input bytes matched the source render, no names remained pending, and the next normal save obtained `assets/Review-i1.png`. The parent parses `limits.json` and `report.json` before classifying the exit or timeout. Both files are written directly, so interruption can leave a partial file. Check status and guard report/accounting decoding and field validation. Preserve raw files and the original process failure. A missing, truncated or invalid report on exit 0 must fail closed. Recheck valid rejection, timeout during report write, invalid success report and the next save. [Report handling](../../../src/pdfmd/xmp_worker.py#L121-L142), [candidate report write](../../../src/pdfmd/primary_xmp.py#L640-L651), [typed error adapter](../../../src/pdfmd/asset_catalog.py#L274-L284).

### AR-T3B-07 — Restart discards the interrupted attempt's diagnostics

**P3, reproduced through `edit_document`.** A controlled child wrote a marker to redirected stdout and paused. The engine was terminated before settlement. The review confirmed the `started` operation, retained log and empty head, then let that child exit. Replaying the same operation through a new Dispatcher with the real worker published the original reserved representation ID and removed the directory that held the interrupted attempt's log. Preparation files are keyed by operation and representation, so a retry opens the same log paths for writing and later removes the shared folder. Keep the durable representation identity and isolate each worker attempt's files. Verify interrupted logs and input survive a successful same-operation retry and that the accepted result uses only its own attempt's output and report. [Preparation path](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L523-L528), [file creation and cleanup](../../../src/pdfmd/xmp_worker.py#L111-L144).

**Checks and disposition.** All 32 focused engine tests in `test_xmp_worker.py`, `test_enriched_save.py` and `test_enriched_save_review.py` passed in 22.14 s. The existing hash-matched frozen executable passed the maintained smoke check, including its enriched PNG save. The development desktop `enriched-save.spec.ts` passed on the existing build in 28.3 s. Its [local run identity](../../ARTIFACT_STORAGE.md#test-executions) is `20261111042929591-19892/enriched-save-IDHVUq`; `desktop-verification.json` records image open/Return, relaunch, edit, relocated ZIP links and matching embedded records. The failure probes above used temporary synthetic inputs and fault injection. Fix and recheck the shared writer lifecycle before relying on it for T4. The [work register](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#context-capability-and-delivery-work) owns disposition. Publisher-source acceptance, cross-process allocation, schema 1 transition, packaged Electron and the T5 matrix retain their existing scope. No provider work, application fix or Git mutation was performed.

**Documentation impact.** The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) explains current handle ownership, report handling and retry paths. The [integration discussion](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t3b-review-and-open-integration-choices) retains the selected worker boundary and links its implementation limits. The [T3b ticket](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3b) now routes to the child implementation and remaining checks. The [continuation route](../../CURRENT_STATE.md#focus-and-resume-route) links this review and leaves the next implementation decision explicit. The README, project layout and adopted asset contracts were checked for dependent claims. The repository document checker reported zero errors.

### AR-T3B-08 — Fixed memory limit rejects a valid large PNG

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. The user's sizing comment reported input/peak memory/time tuples in MiB/MiB/seconds of `2.9/26/0.3`, `11.4/69/0.4`, `25.8/141/0.7` and `37.1/198/1.3`. Fixture identities, the timing definition and raw logs were not supplied with the comment. The approximate `13 MiB + 5 × encoded file size` fit describes these points and puts the 256 MiB crossover near 49 MiB for that profile. It does not establish a universal cutoff or usage distribution.

The review independently called the unchanged `enrich_bounded` Windows development worker with a seeded 4,300 × 4,300 RGB MuPDF PNG and the `test_xmp_worker.py` fixture values in a temporary directory. The image was 55,491,309 bytes (52.9 MiB), SHA-256 `fd4debd21d71fb193a0107d08ec26b281427327e0392308ac2db4d0d3c013753`. At 256 MiB the worker exited 1 in 0.596 s with `MemoryError` in stderr, no timeout and byte-exact retained input. At 512 MiB the same image and values passed writer postchecks and the parent hash checks. The output opened in MuPDF. Peak Job memory was 289,820,672 bytes (276.4 MiB) and the elapsed time including reopen was 1.184 s. Both runs used 5 s Job CPU and 15 s wall limits. No independent CPU measurement, publisher PDF, full save or 100 MiB image was exercised by this probe.

The reproduction establishes a worker-side memory boundary for valid generated PNGs. The worker consumes encoded image bytes. A 1,200 DPI raw raster calculation does not give the PNG file size. The enclosing save also reads source and output bytes in the engine outside the worker Job. The [sizing discussion](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#writer-budget-sizing) owns the unselected policy and proposed checks.

## T3b writer lifecycle and budget correction

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. The user selected fixing AR-T3B-05 to 07 from the [bounded writer review](#t3b-bounded-writer-review) first, then measuring and sizing writer memory for [AR-T3B-08](#ar-t3b-08--fixed-memory-limit-rejects-a-valid-large-png). The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) explains the mechanism, and the [sizing decision](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#writer-budget-sizing) records the rule and what remains open. No provider call or Git mutation was made.

**Corrections.**
- **AR-T3B-05.** The engine owns the kill-on-close Job (`EngineJob`). The worker is created suspended, assigned and then resumed (`NtResumeProcess`), and also waits for a one-byte stdin gate. A wall timeout terminates the Job, and engine death closes the handle, which kills the worker. Suspended creation closes a race found during the check: a venv `python.exe` launcher could otherwise start its interpreter before assignment (the Job accounting showed two processes).
- **AR-T3B-06.** The exit status and timeout are classified first. The report is parsed defensively and trusted only when consistent with the status and the output hashes. Partial, missing or invalid reports fail closed as `process_failed`, `invalid_report` or `output_mismatch`, keeping the raw files.
- **AR-T3B-07.** Each launch uses its own `attempt-*` folder; success removes only that attempt, and the representation folder once empty.
- **AR-T3B-08.** Memory is `max(256 MiB, 64 MiB + 8 × encoded bytes)` with a 2 GiB ceiling, and inputs over the ceiling are rejected before launch (`over_budget`). CPU 5 s and wall 15 s are unchanged. Limit failures name the limit reached. The engine now records the Job's peak memory and charged user and kernel CPU.

**Measurement** (`tools/qualification/measure_writer_budget.py`, run `.local/t3b-budget-20261111-02/`, 4 GiB measurement ceiling):

| Case | Encoded input | Worker peak | User / kernel CPU | Wall |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Seeded-noise MuPDF PNG 2000² | 11.4 MiB | 69.8 MiB | 0.12 / 0.08 s | 0.30 s |
| PNG 4300² (the reviewer's case) | 52.9 MiB | 277.4 MiB | 0.56 / 0.22 s | 0.79 s |
| PNG 6000² | 103.0 MiB | 528.5 MiB | 0.92 / 0.38 s | 1.46 s |
| DCT JPEG with ~15 MiB ExtendedXMP of 830,000 tiny nodes | 14.3 MiB | 1,117.8 MiB | 24.39 / 0.88 s | 25.3 s |

A whole `edit_document` save of the 103 MiB PNG, run in a fresh process after fixture creation and rendering, peaked at 581.7 MiB engine commit (437.9 MiB working set) plus the worker's 528.5 MiB. Run `-01` measured the save in the same process as the fixture creation and render. Its engine figure (1,149 MiB) is therefore not attributable to the save, and its worker used the default 256 MiB and failed as expected.

**Tested identities (SHA-256).** `xmp_worker.py` `548343d4e48dbde6b84185190bc8fc5f59f278ee08193e2338556e43096c9736`; `asset_catalog.py` `27f7809e9f01e52d703124bacc9094d074c38dc39c5de68c16cfaeb4c747ad0a`; `test_xmp_worker.py` `c6c419a1f532ebdb69ca50b13218fc26521e0b95bf0823628dfb42198d1e14fb`; `measure_writer_budget.py` `6386fb531e07a711d50e5945ca94519ba3937eb9fc2be178f468849968267a79`. `documents.py`, `primary_xmp.py` and the build scripts are unchanged from the [bounded writer process](#t3b-bounded-writer-process). The frozen executable is `1b2ba00e337a8b7d50ee057ead0b4afce31af4b106dda54877b249a6c2b683e6` (local build output).

**Checks.**
- **Worker controls** (`test_xmp_worker.py`, 18 tests):
  - accounting is recorded, and success removes only its attempt;
  - a valid rejection keeps its input;
  - a 25.8 MB PNG is accepted at 256 MiB but stopped at 96 MiB with `MemoryError`;
  - a hung worker is terminated at a 1 s wall limit;
  - a probe child confirmed it was inside the engine's Job before the gate opened; after a hard kill of the engine process, it was killed before writing its completion marker, and its input was retained;
  - five malformed or partial report shapes failed closed with the original exit status, and a timeout during the report write is a timeout;
  - a partial report through `edit_document` became `ASSET_ENRICHMENT_FAILED` with no pending names, and the next save succeeded;
  - an interrupted attempt's log and input survived a successful same-operation retry, whose published bytes come from its own attempt;
  - the worker command is a separate process.
- **Sizing tests:**
  - budget values at the floor, mid-range and ceiling;
  - the 52.9 MiB and ~103 MiB PNGs accepted under the sized budget, below their limits;
  - over-ceiling rejection before launch, with no attempt folder;
  - the DOM-heavy JPEG rejected with a named resource limit and the "very many nodes" explanation. In isolation the memory limit fired; in the full documents group, run alongside other load, the 15 s wall limit fired first, so the test accepts any named limit.
- **All enriched-path tests.** 46 passed (worker, enriched save and review suites).
- **Frozen build.** Rebuilt, and `--help` and `check-frozen-engine.py` passed, including an enriched save through the suspended, gated `--xmp-prepare` worker. The engine-death probe ran in development with the same `EngineJob` code; it was not repeated with the frozen executable.
- **Standalone readback.** Run `.local/t3b-readback-20261111-06/`, version `6a45b811bf4b418487f2790a007be8b4`: both images passed with ExifTool 13.55, and 13.61 returned the same records.
- **Desktop.** `enriched-save.spec.ts` passed.
- **Engine groups.** Diagnostics (83 s), execution (275 s), transport (277 s) and context (1,088 s) passed in the full run. Documents failed there only on the DOM-heavy limit assertion described above; after that correction, documents passed (86 s). Logs are under `.local/t3b-groups/`.

**Limits.**
- The noise PNGs are a worst case for PNG size per pixel, not a distribution of real sources.
- The declared minimum host, simultaneous workers, the engine's own copies outside the Job, the POSIX fallback, packaged Electron, publisher sources and the T5 matrix remain open.

## T3b streaming and writer pool

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. The user required concurrent saves and deferred cross-platform limits. The user then selected streaming the engine's image copies first, followed by a pooled, parallel writer, with the proposal's defaults. The [host and concurrency discussion](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#concurrency-engine-copies-and-host-memory-discussion-2026-11-11) records the decision, and the [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) explains the mechanism. No provider call or Git mutation was made.

**Changes.**
- **Streaming.** New `Store` helpers handle image bytes in 1 MiB chunks:
  - `file_digest` and a chunked `_object_intact`;
  - `verify_object`, a streamed integrity check;
  - `copy_object`, a chunked copy that verifies its hash;
  - `blob_from_file`, which registers a file without loading it;
  - `head`, which reads only the leading bytes.

  `xmp_worker.prepare_object` copies the store object into the attempt folder and returns the verified output path, which the save registers with `blob_from_file`. `_write_version` copies image and original members into the version folder for both schema 1 and schema 2 saves. `_ensure_enriched` replaces `_enriched_bytes`, with streamed verification and restore. The page-frame check opens the source PDF by path.
- **Writer pool.** An engine-wide `WriterPool` has capacity `clamp(30% of physical RAM, 1.5 GiB, 8 GiB)` and at most `logical CPUs − 1` workers. Admission reserves each worker's full memory limit and is first come, first served; CPU and wall limits start after admission. An image over this host's pool is rejected as `over_budget` before any attempt exists. On the development host (95.9 GiB RAM, 6 logical CPUs) the pool is 8,192 MiB with 5 workers.
- **Parallel saves.** `_prepare_images` submits a save's creates to the pool at once and registers results in request order. It reuses an intact prepared blob from an earlier attempt of the same operation.

**Measurements** (`measure_writer_budget.py`, final run `.local/t3b-budget-20261111-05/`). Worker figures are unchanged: 69.7, 277.6 and 528.7 MiB peaks for the 11.4, 52.9 and 103 MiB PNGs, and 1,118 MiB with 26 s of CPU for the DOM-heavy JPEG.

Fresh-process whole-save engine peak commit:

| Save | Engine peak commit | Working set |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 103 MiB PNG before streaming (run `-02`) | 581.7 MiB | 437.9 MiB |
| 103 MiB PNG after image streaming (run `-03`) | 375.6 MiB | 231.6 MiB |
| 103 MiB PNG after also opening the PDF by path (runs `-04`, `-05`) | 284.0 / 283.1 MiB | 133.3 / 132.8 MiB |
| 0.7 MiB PNG, same code | 283.7 MiB | 133.2 MiB |

The two measured fresh single-image saves peaked at roughly 284 MiB of engine commit after streaming. A later [base edit probe](#ar-t3b-10--edits-buffer-all-images-in-the-base-version) found an eager read of the saved base. See the [correction and rerun](#t3b-streaming-and-writer-pool-correction).

**Tested identities (SHA-256).**

| File | SHA-256 |
| --- | --- |

**Checks.**

  - a 3-worker cap held for six requests;

After the user freed disk space, the context group was rerun on the same code and passed (1,106 s). Logs are under `.local/t3b-groups/`.

## T3b streaming and writer pool review

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. Standalone review of the Windows development working tree at base `a88f1ba`, including the untracked worker and tests. The worker, document, storage, catalog and test hashes match the [streaming increment's recorded identities](#t3b-streaming-and-writer-pool). The selected `primary_xmp.py` still matches the qualified artifact. Review coverage was the engine-side streaming, worker admission and handoff to immutable publication, including an edit of an existing saved version.

The [correction and rerun](#t3b-streaming-and-writer-pool-correction) records the later implementation and results for these three findings.

### AR-T3B-09 — Concurrent first saves create separate writer pools

**P2, reproduced with controlled scheduling.** Two tasks submitted enriched saves against the same store in a fresh process. The probe paused the second physical-memory query until the first save reached worker launch, using 8 GiB and two logical CPUs to make the configured cap one worker. Both saves succeeded with real workers. Both admissions held live reservations in different pool objects at the same time. Each pool reported one running worker and a limit of one.

`pool()` checks `POOL` before constructing the pool and stores the result without synchronization. The later initializer can replace the pool while the earlier one still has active reservations. Both the aggregate memory allowance and worker count can therefore be exceeded. The existing concurrency tests install a preconstructed pool, so they cover admission on one instance and skip the cold-start path. Host-wide memory pressure and the frequency of the interleaving in ordinary use were not measured.

**Repair check.** Establish one synchronized initialization per engine process, or initialize the pool before serving any save. A cold concurrent-save check should observe a single pool and enforce its combined worker and byte limits while allowing overlapping saves when there is capacity. Retain FIFO behavior and start the worker deadline at admission.

### AR-T3B-10 — Edits buffer all images in the base version

**P2, reproduced on a normal edit.** An enriched base held two separate MuPDF-derived PNG assets of 12,595,145 and 12,595,139 bytes. An edit removing both links called `Path.read_bytes()` for both saved images. Python allocations peaked at 25,266,302 bytes during the edit, and the resulting version reopened with zero assets. This allocation is separate from native PDF and worker allocations.

`Documents.edit` calls `version` for the selected base. `_verified_version` reads and retains all members, including image and original streams, to check their hashes. `version` uses only the parsed envelope. The code path is shared by schema 1 and schema 2. Concurrent edits can each incur this cost, even if their next versions omit those members. The fresh single-image measurements still describe their original path.

**Repair check.** Give the edit/base reader streamed member verification while keeping its envelope and task/version identity checks. Test a base containing multiple large assets and an edit that removes them. Also damage a base member the next version would omit and verify rejection preserves the candidate and head. Keep actual preview and export byte delivery within their separate contracts.

### AR-T3B-11 — Publication can register bytes the writer did not validate

**P2, reproduced by fault injection.** A real bounded worker produced and validated an enriched PNG. Immediately after `run_attempt` verified the file against the report, the probe substituted the exact unenriched input PNG at the same path. The normal edit returned success. The saved version reopened with the substituted SHA-256 in its asset record and manifest. The published bytes matched the original input and lacked the new provenance. The fault was injected between validation and registration. Incidence in ordinary use is unknown.

`run_attempt` returns only a path. `blob_from_file` hashes that path again and treats its current content as the expected identity. The later immutable-file checks then faithfully publish whichever bytes were registered. This violates the [finalized-byte and provenance boundary](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#primary-xmp-enrichment-boundary).

**Repair check.** Carry the writer-verified digest into streamed object registration and require the staged and registered bytes to match it. Keep the failed attempt and candidate available and leave the previous head intact on mismatch. Retest exact valid readback and an injected substitution at that handoff, including verification that no version or successful receipt was published.

### Review result and continuation

The focused existing XMP worker, enriched save, enriched save review and portable-link save suites passed (85 tests). The three Windows probes above are retained with their commands and output under ignored `.local/t3b-pool-review-80c080eb/`. These checks exercised synthetic saves and reopened versions. Existing independent reader, desktop and frozen-engine runs retain their earlier scope. The review did not rerun them or test publisher sources, a physical minimum-spec host, cross-process allocation or the destination matrix.

The concurrent-save bounds and validated image identity need these local corrections before accepting the save path for T4. T4 will reuse the same base reads and worker-to-store handoff. The bounded repair directions above preserve the adopted defaults and writer candidate. Keep host sizing, real publisher validation and the agent-facing flow on their existing routes. [The work register](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#context-capability-and-delivery-work) owns selection and revisit conditions.

**Documentation impact.** The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) distinguishes streamed copies from base loading, corrects the invoked helper and links the pool and registration findings. The [concurrency discussion](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#concurrency-engine-copies-and-host-memory-discussion-2026-11-11) retains the user's selected order and defaults. The [T3b ticket](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3b) routes to the review and the settled lifecycle checks. The [asset contract](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#save-and-immutable-dependency-preparation) still requires validated finalized bytes. The README's saved-preview claims and Stage B/D outcomes do not depend on the streaming measurement or the pool initialization. The repository documentation checker and Git whitespace check passed. The continuation route leads from CURRENT_STATE through the observed counterexamples to the recommended work and adopted rules.

2026-11-11, Asia/Singapore. The user selected the T3b cross-process allocation work (the AR-10 boundary control in the [ticket's remaining scope](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3b)) after the [recommendation](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#next-step-recommendation-after-the-ar-t3b-09-to-11-correction). Code basis: `023ea37` on `codex/r01cal-a03-delivery-01`. Synthetic task stores from `tests/fixtures/pdf/rgb-jpeg.pdf`, real bounded writer workers, no provider call. The probe, its runs and logs are under ignored `.local/t3b-xproc-20261111/`. The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) owns the mechanism.

**Probe on the unchanged code (`probe.py` run `run-01`).** Two spawned engine processes opened one store and each saved one enriched create of `Same label` through its own `Dispatcher`.

- *Different operations.* A reserved `Same label-p58.png` and B, while A's reservation was pending, `Same label-p58（2）.png`. B published first; A failed with `HEAD_CONFLICT` and its name was released. No path was bound to two occurrences, and a later create received the released `Same label-p58.png`. B's published name keeps its `（2）` although no version uses the unsuffixed name. This follows from reserving durably before publication: a pending reservation is a real conflict when the suffix is chosen. It is recorded as observed behavior, not a defect.
- *Same operation (AR-T3B-12, P3, reproduced).* Both processes read the absent preparation record before either wrote it. A wrote its identities and reserved `Same label-p58.png`; B then overwrote the record with new occurrence identities, found the name held by A's occurrence and reserved `Same label-p58（2）.png`. A published; B found the operation done and returned A's receipt, so both callers received the same correct version. B's `（2）` stayed `pending` under the completed operation, and the preparation record named B's unpublished occurrence. A later, genuinely new occurrence of the same name received `Same label-p58（3）.png`, although no version uses `（2）`. This contradicts AR-10's next-available-suffix rule; the published version and receipt were unaffected. How often two attempts of one operation run at once in ordinary use (an HTTP or desktop resubmission while the first attempt is still running) was not measured.

**Correction.** `_save_enriched` now reads or creates the `asset_preparation` record in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction, so concurrent attempts of an operation share its identities. When publication finds the operation already done, the same transaction releases any name still pending under that operation before returning the original receipt. `_release` shares that step.

**Checks.**
- New tests in `test_enriched_save.py`, with `allocation_child.py` running each engine as a separate Python process synchronized through files. Each process pauses at name reservation after its preparation record exists; A reserves first, then B.
  - *Different operations:* exactly one save publishes, the other returns `HEAD_CONFLICT`, the winner's name is `assigned` and the loser's `released`, and a later create receives the loser's released name.
  - *Same operation:* both callers receive one identical receipt and one version, the only name record is `Same label-p58.png` `assigned`, the preparation record's occurrence matches the published one, and a later create receives `（2）`.
  - *Leftover release (in process):* an attempt that reaches publication after another attempt of its operation published returns the original receipt and releases an injected stale pending name.
- On the pre-correction `documents.py` the leftover-release test fails (`pending` instead of `released`). Both cross-process tests pass there too: they pause after the preparation record exists, which is past the overwrite window. They check the corrected end state; the deterministic reproduction of the overwrite is the probe above. A test pause between the read and write is no longer possible, because both now sit inside one write transaction that the other process cannot enter.
- The two cross-process tests passed in three repeated runs. The full `tests/engine/documents` group passed (195 tests), and so did `tests/engine/execution` (236 tests).

**Tested identities (SHA-256).** `documents.py` `ef2952506c05616529f2a462c84ea8431e56f70d6bcad8f59bacf4183aaed0b4`; `test_enriched_save.py` `f3bed9e239e995b71ba15dcc548f7e703effcc1d7869d9d0a0d253968d8534ae`; `allocation_child.py` `5a581af2b4d0bb7e124be27639ab49416e575172ab9ec1cbbd0f1491893f443c`; probe `c2c204e730f641e3e2b8284a1d71ada6f33910652d25c160c2e548ad487a2912`.

**Limits.** The processes shared one Windows NTFS store; network filesystems, where SQLite locking is not dependable, were not tested. Restart of an interrupted attempt keeps its [earlier evidence](#t3b-review-correction). The frozen engine, desktop specs and readback were not rerun for this engine-only change. The published-suffix behavior after a failed concurrent reservation is unchanged and remains for the user to accept or revisit under AR-10.

## T3b cross-process correction review

2026-11-12, Asia/Singapore. Reviewed clean code at `d65a0a4`, using `023ea37` as the comparison base for AR-T3B-12. The transaction around preparation identity lookup and insertion closes the original lost-update window. The publication fallback releases a pending name of the same operation and returns the existing receipt when the receipt is still present. All 195 document-engine tests passed in 119.48 seconds, including the three AR-10 tests. The two process tests coordinate after preparation lookup and cover the recorded end state. The review exercised earlier and later timing windows using two processes on isolated Windows stores, a synthetic one-page MuPDF PDF and real bounded workers. The probe and logs are under ignored `.local/t3b-xproc-review-d2393380/`.

See the [receipt and preparation correction](#t3b-receipt-and-preparation-correction) for the later implementation and rerun of these findings.

**HTTP control.** Two app instances in separate processes opened the same store. While the first same-task edit held the task file lock at name reservation, the other request with the same idempotency key received HTTP 409. The first request returned HTTP 200 and a saved version. A same-key retry after lock release returned HTTP 200 with the identical response. The following findings concern direct overlapping `Dispatcher.execute` calls like the native cross-process test driver uses. The normal HTTP edit and agent runtime acquire the task lock before dispatch.

### AR-T3B-13 — An overlapping dispatch can overwrite the accepted save receipt

**P2 in the unguarded native path, reproduced with two schedules.** In the first probe, pause the second attempt immediately before `Dispatcher.execute` stores `started`. Let the first attempt publish and project its receipt, then release the second attempt. Its unconditional upsert overwrote `done`. The second caller raised an immutable-result conflict and a fresh exact retry reported that the retained result did not match the arguments. The saved head still reopened. The accepted version's result blob already existed, but the operation row was left at `started` and the projection's metadata lacked the arguments hash that recovery expected for a staged result.

In the second probe, pause one attempt before an injected local preparation failure. Let its peer publish, then pause the peer before it projects the successful receipt to the tool-result blob. Release the failing attempt. `Dispatcher.settle` wrote the failure under the same immutable result key and replaced the already committed operation receipt. The successful peer then hit an immutable-result conflict. Exact retry returned the recorded preparation failure even though the saved version existed and reopened. The injected failure represents one possible local preparation failure. Its frequency in ordinary use is unknown.

**Consequence and next check.** Same-task concurrency outside the existing writer lock can break immutable replay across the Dispatcher lifecycle. The atomic preparation fix by itself leaves both of these entry and settlement windows. Keep the existing per-task writer boundary for same-task edits and prove all supported native callers acquire it. If overlapping direct attempts are needed, give the operation one claim and terminal result owner across start, failure settlement, publication and receipt projection. Replay a successful save after both schedules and verify one receipt, one head and identical returned bytes. Different tasks can still save concurrently.

### AR-T3B-14 — A delayed preparation can reset its committed state

**P3 in the unguarded native path, reproduced.** Pause the second attempt after it starts and enters the edit, at the source-page check before the preparation transaction. Let the first attempt publish, then release the second. Both callers and a later retry received the identical successful receipt. The shared `asset_preparation` record ended at `preparing` without its committed version link. `_save_enriched` reads the existing record under the write lock but writes a fresh preparing record even when the operation already committed. The publication fallback returns the receipt without restoring that preparation record. Version visibility and the assigned name survived.

**Consequence and next check.** The lost commit link makes preparation state disagree with the published version. Preserve the committed preparation under the same-task writer boundary or check terminal operation/preparation state before updating it if direct overlap is supported. Verify that a delayed exact attempt leaves the same committed version link and releases only genuinely leftover pending names.

### Cross-process review result and continuation

The AR-T3B-12 correction addresses the original identity overwrite and leaked suffix. Accept that bounded result for the current task-locked app path. The same-operation direct-dispatch evidence covers specific schedules and cannot establish general concurrent replay. Retain the task-writer boundary for T4. Treat support for overlapping same-task direct callers as a separate choice before depending on it. The published suffix after a failed competing reservation remains on its existing AR-10 decision route. The frozen, desktop and standalone reader checks retain their recorded code identities and were not repeated here.

**Documentation impact.** The [engine guide](../../guides/DOCUMENT_ENGINE.md#enriched-creation-path-t3b) now states the task-lock boundary and links the direct-dispatch limits. The [T3b ticket](tickets.md#r01cal-a03-delivery-01-t3b) routes to the review. The [work register](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#context-capability-and-delivery-work) owns the new findings and their selection conditions. The asset design already calls for task-writer ordering, and the README's preview behavior and the phase acceptance conditions do not depend on unrestricted concurrent dispatch. Documentation and Git whitespace checks passed.

## T4 residual correction review

2026-11-12, Asia/Singapore. Standalone review of T4 on `codex/r01cal-a03-delivery-01`, using `763d0f2` and the working-tree shared-page increment. The three reviewed code/test hashes and the three retained desktop ZIP hashes match the [shared-page evidence](#t4-shared-printed-page-correction). The retained Electron and ExifTool reports identify the same original, corrected and later-linked versions. A fresh focused engine run passed both existing single-occurrence and shared-page tests. The desktop and independent-reader runs are retained evidence from the preceding increment.

### AR-T4-01 — One correction per edit is stricter than one per occurrence

The [edit contract](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#document-edit-api-and-operation-semantics) allows one explicit correction for each occurrence in a document operation. The [correction loop](../../../src/pdfmd/documents.py#L538-L556) uses a single flag for the whole request. A synthetic HTTP edit with two valid corrections to distinct existing occurrences returned `ASSET_FACT_INVALID` and “This edit supports one occurrence correction at a time.” The saved head and version count stayed unchanged, the candidate matched the request, and no preparation record was added. A duplicate-occurrence request also failed. A single-correction control succeeded and preserved the other occurrence's representation. This blocks publishing two explicitly requested corrections in one version. Separate saves can reach the same eventual facts, but publish an intermediate version. No real-task incidence was assessed. See the [work register](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#context-capability-and-delivery-work) for disposition.

The same synthetic HTTP probe confirmed the known shared-source limitation: a `source_update.title` supported by visible PDF evidence failed once the catalog held occurrences. Head, version count and preparation count stayed unchanged and the candidate was retained. The report and isolated task store are under ignored `.local/t4-plan-review-yxk7vhl7/`.

**Scoped assessment.** The reviewed evidence supports the single-occurrence and shared printed-page increments on the development path. Shared source metadata, per-occurrence batching and the remaining correction lifecycle checks keep T4 open. The installed product, publisher-source cases and destination matrix retain their prior evidence limits.

**Documentation impact.** The [draft T4 acceptance](spec.md#proposed-t4-completion-checks) and [local ticket proposal](tickets.md#proposed-t4-follow-up-tickets) connect the remaining cases to the adopted contract. The [discussion](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#review-and-proposed-t4-completion) records the ordering rationale, and the work register and continuation link to their owners. The design already describes the intended per-occurrence rule. The engine guide's single-occurrence and shared-page sections describe the reviewed implementation. Repository documentation and Git whitespace checks passed.

The synthetic PDF uses the same DCT JPEG at two positions on physical page 1 and at a third position on page 2. The regression cites one retained image extraction and separate page evidence for the third placement.

**Atomic corrections and shared facts.** The new regression reproduced [AR-T4-01](#ar-t4-01--one-correction-per-edit-is-stricter-than-one-per-occurrence) on `f962131`. The corrected engine applied both explicit naming changes and the printed-label patch in one version. The two affected images received one fresh representation each, with their own corrected descriptions and the shared printed label `59`. Their operative destinations changed together. The page 2 occurrence kept its binding, embedded record and exported bytes. A following edit composed two explicit image-title patches with a shared source-title patch. It produced exactly one new representation for each affected occurrence, including the page 2 occurrence affected only by the shared source patch. The assigned names, operative destinations, code literal, decoded pixels and retained original bytes were preserved in that edit.

**Duplicate and history controls.** A duplicate left occurrence ID rejected an edit containing a no-op first correction, a valid right correction and a shared page patch. The candidate stayed exact, the edit created no preparation and the head and version count stayed at the base. A fresh engine instance reopened and exported the three selected versions. The original export members remained byte-exact, the saved references and each embedded record agreed with the selected version, and retrying the rejected edit returned its original receipt with the newer head intact.

**Independent readback.** ExifTool 13.55 recovered all three matching structured records and identifiers from renamed standalone JPEGs from the final ZIP. The two explicit image titles and the repeat's derived label stayed distinct in `dc:title`. The readable XMP description and structured record reported the same shared source title in each image.

### AR-T4-02 — Separate extractions of one original stream trigger an inventory warning

Observed on the T4-A checkpoint `f962131` while constructing the fixture, before applying a correction. Extracting the shared image stream separately from pages 1 and 2 produced two original evidence refs for the same export path and bytes. The creation save and ZIP preserved the exact original stream. The saved inventory maps that path to one of those refs, and the [assessment](../../../src/pdfmd/asset_references.py#L523-L529) compares it against each occurrence's declared ref. Reopen and export reported `inventory_mismatch` for the two page 1 citations despite matching paths and SHA-256. The affected version is `6560d9a2144846f284ea83051859127b`; the original's SHA-256 is `2efc958613d00a64a6638bc53a8a39cfa0222995b11ec9981956cc6cf4327b5b`.

The T4-B regression used one retained extraction and separate page-location evidence to verify correction batching. The independent-extraction warning was open at T4-B closeout. Both fixtures, the ZIP manifest check and the warning details are retained under ignored `.local/t4-b-multiple-corrections-a4946912/`. See the subsequent [T4-C correction](#ar-t4-02-correction).

## T4-C correction lifecycle

2026-11-12, Asia/Singapore. T4-B and its records were checkpointed locally as `28199ab` on `codex/r01cal-a03-delivery-01`. The [collision, history and recovery case](spec.md#t4-completion-checks) ran through the normal HTTP edit route on the development engine and a matching frozen engine. Two occurrences of the same JPEG occupy physical pages 1 and 2, each visibly labelled `59`. The fixture uses two separate image extractions and one verified original ZIP member.

**Collision and restart.** A test-only fault exited the server after the corrected files were prepared and before SQLite publication. The candidate, prepared image, representation ID and pending `Shared view-p59 (2).jpeg` reservation survived. The unpublished version ID never entered the saved-version catalog. After restarting normally, the same request and key published with the reserved suffix, representation ID and exact prepared bytes. The pre-existing `Shared view-p59.jpeg` kept its association, embedded provenance and exact bytes. The corrected occurrence kept its ID. Its inline embed and hyperlink definition resolved to the suffixed image, and the unrelated literal stayed exact.

**Historical base and stale settlement.** An explicit rebuild from the original base used the corrected version as expected head. It recovered the original version's paths, catalog, Markdown, preview, display bytes and originals. A second interruption left a pending correction to `Alternate view-p59.jpeg`. After a later edit advanced head, retry of the interrupted request returned `HEAD_CONFLICT`, released the pending name and retained the candidate and prepared image. Replays of the successful collision correction and the settled stale failure returned their exact envelopes without another publication or head change. The original version's post-restart export matched its pre-correction member bytes.

**Desktop, relocation and picture fidelity.** The Windows Electron 45.0.0 development app reopened the frozen engine's store after close and relaunch. Both native image links opened the selected image bytes and Return restored the selected document for the original, corrected and rebuilt versions. Every desktop ZIP member matched its engine export. In a relocated extraction the corrected HTML opened both relative `file:` links in a separate local Electron window and decoded each image at 64 × 48. ExifTool 13.55 read matching complete structured records and both IDs from independently renamed images. Physical pages stayed distinct under the repeated printed label. The decoded pixels and the JPEG bytes outside the primary XMP group matched the retained DCT original across the three exports.

## AR-10 published-suffix verification

The [user's selection](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#ar-10-published-suffix-policy-selected) adopts the [published-name rule](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#collision-only-allocation-and-identity). On Windows 10/NTFS, the focused synthetic test used two engine processes dispatching to one isolated store. The parent held A after it reserved `Same label-i1.png` and let B reserve and publish `Same label-i1（2）.png` first. At the first export, A's claim was pending and B's was assigned. Releasing A produced `HEAD_CONFLICT`, preserved its candidate and marked the unsuffixed name released. B remained the only published version.

The normal version and export routes reopened the winner with its original embed and link, occurrence and representation IDs, and the same embedded basename and suffix. Every ZIP member matched the snapshot taken before A failed. A later eligible edit took `Same label-i1.png` and preserved the suffixed image bytes. Re-exporting the winner after that edit matched the original snapshot. Exact retries returned the original successful and failed receipts, with the newer head intact and two versions total. The synthetic task recorded zero provider attempts and requests.

Winner `e9191417d96041769815c9035feaea27` produced three archives with SHA-256 `5882313891609212b1b83cbaa2c23f21a41310c5f1d87ee374e0cd863bee5906`. **Scoped result.** The published-suffix case passed locally. The controlled race exercises native dispatch, with normal API reopen and export. The [T5 canonical ZIP](#t5-local-integration-on-windows-10) retained its recorded digest. Windows 11, Ubuntu and macOS remain open under the [destination disposition](#destination-disposition).

**Documentation impact.** The [design](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#collision-only-allocation-and-identity) records the adopted rule, the [spec](spec.md#ar-10--collision-allocation-retries-and-distinct-occurrences) adds the discriminating case, and the [T5 ticket](tickets.md#t5-local-execution) and [continuation](../../CURRENT_STATE.md#focus-and-resume-route) route to this evidence and the remaining checks. Repository documentation and Git whitespace checks passed.

## T5 offline publisher-source check

2026-11-12, Asia/Singapore. The user [selected one offline publisher-source check](../../discussions/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#t5-offline-publisher-source-check-selected). The local News Writing PDF matched the [recorded source identity](../../verification/20261106_1408_R01CAL_TASK_A.md#identities-and-preserved-conditions). The normal import route made a byte-exact copy in a fresh Windows 10/NTFS store. The [same frozen engine](#t5-local-integration-on-windows-10) was used for both launches. The operator supplied evidence-backed facts for a partial document. The task had no configured route, zero provider attempts and zero request records.

**Page evidence and names.** Visual review of the full pages established the absence of a printed number on the cover and the exact printed numbers on physical pages 11 and 12. Poppler reported JBIG2 segment warnings during rendering. The product's MuPDF page renders independently showed both footer labels. The edit used task-owned page render references as the evidence basis.

| Physical page | Observed printed label | Delivered basename |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Absent on the front cover | `封面-i1.jpeg` |
| 11 | `1` at the lower right | `第一章首页-p1.png` |
| 12 | `2` at the lower left | `第一章续页-p2.png` |

**Reopen, export and standalone provenance.** After terminating the engine process and launching a new one against the same store, the normal selected-version route reopened `544b2f8a77bc491c8b611a83c05a8567`. Its resource hashes and Markdown and HTML link and embed destinations matched the three assets. The normal export route produced an eight-member ZIP, SHA-256 `8b9611e04294bd10f48c0a1b4ae51fd4e64d32bc02ac55d243eaf4872d2ea16c`. The extracted files matched the saved version and manifest. ExifTool 13.55 read each image after copying it alone to an empty directory and renaming it. The readable and structured fields agreed on the visible source title and publisher, the unknown full citation, and each physical page and printed label. The cover's structured label was `none` with a null value and a preserved evidence pointer. The two numbered pages retained `known` and their exact labels. The structured records matched the saved records, including the assigned basenames and source identity. Both PNGs retained all bytes outside the added XMP chunk and decoded to identical pixels.

**Eligible JPEG.** The cover stream at PDF object 2137 is a DCT JPEG with no soft mask, copied unchanged by the extraction tool. It had one StandardXMP packet and no ExtendedXMP or recognized legacy XMP carrier. Its SHA-256, `228660d981f8af0dc7415d84f8b4b1cb06b052a416e7129f2e9cdeb4f715088b`, matched the retained ZIP original. All JPEG bytes outside the primary XMP group matched, including the encoded image and decode parameters. The same decoder returned identical RGB samples at 1968 × 2894. All 17 pre-existing non-allowlisted primary properties matched semantically, including Photoshop history, orientation and the original creation date. ExifTool's all-documents read of the renamed derivative preserved the corresponding original property values and recovered one set of current source fields in the main document, with matching default and Simplified Chinese language alternatives. The original XMP toolkit packet framing was replaced. The reader reported no warnings and exposed no stale or competing source field. The source had no pre-existing competing source fields, so this result supplies real-source merge evidence within the [primary XMP boundary](../../design/ASSET_REFERENCES.md#primary-xmp-enrichment-boundary).

**Scope and retained evidence.** The three-page offline control passed. A real unresolved printed label and a publisher JPEG with pre-existing competing values or legacy XMP remain unexercised. The control used the native engine save/reopen/export path with operator-supplied facts. The [earlier desktop evidence](#t5-local-integration-on-windows-10) covers the synthetic version. The publisher source remained byte-exact and the canonical synthetic `delivery.zip` retained its recorded SHA-256. Windows 11, Ubuntu and macOS remain open under the [destination disposition](#destination-disposition). The private renders, exact source stream, archive, harness, readbacks and report are retained under ignored `.local/t5-publisher-fae6d9e6/`.

**Documentation impact.** The [criterion map](#t5-criterion-evidence-map) records the exercised AR-8 and AR-12 coverage and residual scope. The [T5 ticket](tickets.md#t5-local-execution) links the selected run, the [work register](../../WORK_ITEMS.md#selection-now) routes follow-up through the criterion map, and the [resume route](../../CURRENT_STATE.md#focus-and-resume-route) points to the new evidence. The governing design and phase conditions still apply. Repository documentation and Git whitespace checks passed.
